The results show that the generalized distortion model more faithfully captures physical camera effects and yields an improvement in calibration accuracy. Reprojection error is shown to be a potentially misleading indicator of 3D accuracy, and the proposed ray-based metrics provide a more holistic assessment. An icosahedron calibration target is designed to enrich calibration information for 3D reconstruction together with a ring-feature-based detector. The icosahedral target yields approximately 40% lower mean intersection and more stable calibration across bootstrap trials on synthetic data, while real-data performance demands very tight fabrication tolerances.

</details>


### [100] [VQ-VAD: Vector-quantized Motion Representation Learning for Human-centric Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05069)
*Narges Rashvand,Ghazal Alinezhad Noghre,Shanle Yao,Gabriel Maldonado,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VQ-VAD框架，将矢量量化生成对抗网络（VQ-GAN）从图像生成领域适配到人体关键点序列，构建正常行为的离散运动码本，用于基于姿态的视频异常检测。VQ-VAD在四个基准数据集、三种评估设置（域内、跨域、跨数据集）下均表现优异，在HR-SHT上取得81.83%的域内准确率和76.69%的跨域迁移准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常检测面临异常样本稀缺和监控场景视觉变化大（光照、视角、外观）等挑战。基于姿态的方法可缓解视觉噪声和隐私问题，但现有方法在连续隐空间中建模人类行为，难以学习紧凑的运动模式，限制了鲁棒行为分析的能力。

Method: VQ-VAD将原本用于图像生成的VQ-GAN适配到关键点序列上，构建正常行为的离散运动码本（motion codebook）。模型仅使用正常运动序列训练，通过将观测运动序列映射到码本时的重建误差来识别异常行为。

Result: 在四个异常检测基准上进行了域内、跨域和跨数据集泛化三种评估。域内准确率达81.83%（HR-SHT），从CMU Panoptic跨域迁移至HR-SHT无需重训练即达76.69%，跨数据集鲁棒性也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: VQ-VAD通过离散运动表示学习证明了基于姿态的视频异常检测的有效性，其在域内和跨域场景中的优异表现表明离散码本有助于捕获紧凑的运动模式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) is inherently challenging due to the scarcity of anomalies and the large visual variability in surveillance footage, including changes in lighting, viewpoint, and human appearance. To mitigate visual noise and address privacy concerns, recent work has shifted to pose-based VAD, which focuses on motion dynamics rather than raw video data. However, existing pose-based approaches model human behavior in continuous latent spaces, limiting their ability to learn compact motion patterns necessary for robust behavior analysis. We address this by proposing Vector-Quantized Video Anomaly Detection (VQ-VAD), a novel human-centric anomaly detection framework that learns discrete motion representations. VQ-VAD adapts Vector-Quantized GAN (VQ-GAN), originally developed for image generation, to operate on keypoint sequences and construct a motion codebook of normal behavior. Trained exclusively on normal motion sequences, VQ-VAD detects anomalies by identifying high reconstruction errors when an observed motion sequence cannot be mapped to the learned codebook. We conduct extensive experiments across three complementary evaluation settings, including in-domain, cross-domain, and cross-dataset generalization, on four anomaly detection benchmarks. VQ-VAD achieves strong in-domain accuracy (81.83% on HR-SHT [15]), effective cross-domain transfer from CMU Panoptic [14] (76.69% on HR-SHT [15] without retraining), and competitive cross-dataset robustness. The code base for this work is available at https://github.com/TeCSAR-UNCC/VQ-VAD.

</details>


### [101] [HelloWorld: Enabling Socially Interactive Characters in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05070)
*Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Xuangeng Chu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HelloWorld是一个支持用户与视频世界模型中角色进行社交互动的视频世界模型。用户只需按一下按钮，即可让屏幕中的角色面向镜头做出回应（如转头、挥手、点头或问候）。通过自蒸馏流程微调视频生成模型，并引入无需训练的模块来定位交互发生的时间窗口。论文还构建了HelloWorldBench基准（400个样本）进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽然在生成质量上取得了显著进展，但用户与视频中角色之间缺乏社交互动能力，这是当前视频世界模型的一个重要空白。

Method: 1) 提出自蒸馏（self-distillation）流水线，使用模型自身合成的数据微调视频生成模型，每个合成片段同时包含社交互动和相机运动，使模型在不降低交互质量的前提下学习相机姿态条件化；2) 提出一种无需训练（training-free）的推理模块，通过调制DiT的交叉注意力掩码，使交互相关的文本提示仅关注按钮按下窗口内的帧，从而在时间上定位角色的响应。

Result: 实验表明HelloWorld在交互质量上超越了多种基线方法，同时保持了最先进的画面美学质量和相机姿态跟随性能。

Conclusion: HelloWorld成功实现了视频世界模型中的社交交互能力，填补了该领域空白，并通过自蒸馏和训练无关模块设计在交互质量与生成质量之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable recent progress of video world models, social interaction between users and the characters within these worlds remains unsupported. To fill this gap, we present HelloWorld, a video world model that enables social interaction with in-world characters. With a single button press, users can prompt the on-screen character to respond toward the camera, e.g., turning to the viewer, waving, nodding, or speaking a short greeting. To make these interactions natural, we propose a self-distillation pipeline that finetunes the video generation model on data synthesized by itself. Each synthesized clip contains both social interactions and camera motion, allowing the model to learn camera-pose conditioning without degrading interaction quality. At inference, we further introduce a training-free module that determines when the interaction occurs. Upon a button press, it modulates the cross-attention masks of the DiT so that the interaction-related text prompt attends only to the frames within the press window, temporally localizing the character's response. We further build HelloWorldBench, a 400-sample benchmark with three social interaction metrics alongside three conventional metrics, for evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that HelloWorld surpasses a variety of baselines in interaction quality, while maintaining state-of-the-art picture aesthetics and camera-pose following. Project page: https://github.com/AlayaLab/HelloWorld

</details>


### [102] [Bag-of-Visual-Words for Spatial Mapping of Lung Adenocarcinoma Growth Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05074)
*Darya Ardan,Valentin Oreiller,Henning Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种弱监督的Bag-of-Visual-Words (BoVW)流水线，利用冻结的基础模型嵌入从少量标注感兴趣区域(ROIs)学习视觉词汇表，构建模式原型并在滑动窗口区域上进行最近原型分类，最终在WSI瓦片网格上生成可解释的空间模式图。在87例CPTAC-LUAD患者上，肿瘤/健康分类达到0.974的平衡准确率，组织学分级分类在全部编码器上均优于监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的肺癌腺癌(LUAD)空间映射方法在单个瓦片级别操作，产生的是通用的形态聚类而非临床定义的图案图谱，无法在区域级别解析组织结构上下文。

Method: 1) 从少量标注ROIs中提取冻结的基础模型嵌入；2) 构建BoVW视觉词汇表；3) 将同标签ROIs的BoVW直方图均值作为模式原型；4) 使用Jensen-Shannon散度在滑动窗口区域上进行最近原型分类；5) 将预测投影到WSI瓦片网格上生成空间模式图。

Result: 在87例CPTAC-LUAD患者上，使用三种基础模型编码器和多种词汇表大小进行评估。肿瘤/健康分类最佳配置(H-Optimus-1)达到0.974平衡准确率，接近监督SVM的0.987；二元组织学分级分类中，BoVW流水线在所有编码器上均优于监督基线。

Conclusion: ROI级别的模式分解保留了全局均值池化所衰减的等级相关异质性，BoVW方法能以弱监督方式生成临床可解释的空间模式图，在分级分类任务上优于全局池化基线。

Abstract: Spatial mapping of lung adenocarcinoma (LUAD) growth patterns across whole slide images (WSIs) requires resolving architectural context at the region level, yet existing methods operate at the individual tile level and produce generic morphological clusters rather than clinically defined pattern maps. We propose a weakly supervised Bag-of-Visual-Words (BoVW) pipeline that learns a visual vocabulary from frozen foundation model embeddings extracted from a small set of annotated regions of interest (ROIs). Pattern prototypes are constructed as mean BoVW histograms of same-label ROIs and used for nearest-prototype classification of sliding-window regions under Jensen--Shannon divergence. The resulting predictions are projected onto the WSI tile grid to produce interpretable spatial pattern maps. We evaluate the method on 87 CPTAC-LUAD patients using three foundation model encoders and multiple vocabulary sizes on two clinically motivated tasks. For tumour/healthy classification, the best configuration achieves a balanced accuracy of $0.974$ with H-Optimus-1, approaching the $0.987$ obtained by a supervised SVM trained on mean-pooled WSI embeddings. For binary histologic grade classification, the BoVW pipeline achieves higher balanced accuracy than the supervised baseline for all encoders, suggesting that ROI-level pattern decomposition preserves grade-relevant heterogeneity that is attenuated by global mean pooling.

</details>


### [103] [Lesion Detection in CT with Frozen Self-Distilled Features: SALT, a Spatially Adaptive Label-Guided Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05100)
*Mahmut S. Gokmen,Evan W. Damron,Mitchell A. Klusty,Caroline N. Leach,Emily B. Collier,V. K. Cody Bumgardner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT（Spatially Adaptive Label-guided Temperature），一种空间自适应的自监督预训练目标调制方法。它利用仅在预训练期间可用的弱盒级标签，在编码器的patch网格上定义紧凑区域，在该区域内锐化教师softmax温度并提高掩码patch损失的权重，使病变区域对训练信号的贡献不再与周围实质均等。冻结编码器后在四个CT队列上进行3D病变检测评估，证明该方法在patch级可分离性、3D检测、框质量及无配准/无掩码的探测器自由探针上均优于仅改变视野但不对目标进行条件化的标签引导裁剪基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督预训练目标在空间上是均匀的——教师温度和每个patch的损失权重在图像各处完全相同，导致仅有数个patch宽的病变对训练信号的贡献不高于周围正常实质。先前的工作将视图偏向标注区域，只改变了模型"看到什么"，但没有对目标函数本身施加压力。作者希望直接条件化自蒸馏的目标，使模型在预训练阶段就针对病变区域产生更强的学习信号。

Method: SALT方法在自蒸馏框架中引入空间自适应调制：(1) 利用弱盒级标签（仅预训练阶段可用）在编码器的patch网格上定义紧凑区域；(2) 在该区域内锐化教师softmax温度（temperature sharper），增强教师输出的尖锐度；(3) 同时在该区域内提高掩码patch重建损失的权重。除此之外，目标函数、掩码策略和centering统计量均保持不变，下游使用时编码器是纯特征提取器，无需标签或条件化。

Result: 在四个CT队列上冻结编码器并仅训练轻量级多深度CenterNet风格检测头进行3D病变检测。与架构、预训练数据、训练计划和标签引导裁剪均相同但不含目标条件化的骨干进行机制隔离对比。报告了patch级可分离性、按队列和病变大小分层的3D检测性能、框质量以及探测器自由探针结果——单个冻结patch嵌入无需配准、掩码或微调即可在随访扫描中重新识别病变。

Conclusion: 由于条件化通过空间指示器而非标签语义表达，SALT方法具有通用性，可适配任意弱空间标注。作者在病变检测上进行了实例化和验证，证明通过直接调制自蒸馏目标，能够在不改变模型所见视野的前提下提升病变相关的表示质量。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining objectives are spatially uniform: the teacher temperature and the per-patch loss weight are identical everywhere in the image, so a lesion a few patches wide contributes no more to the training signal than the surrounding parenchyma. Prior work biases the views toward annotated regions, which changes what the model sees but adds no pressure on the objective. We instead condition the targets of self-distillation, a method we call SALT (Spatially Adaptive Label-guided Temperature). Weak, box-derived labels, available only during pretraining, define a compact region on the encoder's patch grid, inside which the teacher's softmax temperature is sharpened and the masked-patch loss is up-weighted. The objectives, the masking policy and the centering statistics are otherwise unchanged, and at every downstream use the encoder is a plain feature extractor with no labels and no conditioning. We evaluate by freezing the encoder and training only a lightweight multi-depth CenterNet-style head, detecting lesions in 3D on four CT cohorts, and we isolate the mechanism against a backbone identical in architecture, pretraining data, schedule and label-guided cropping but with no target conditioning. We report patch-level separability, 3D detection stratified by cohort and by lesion size, box quality, and a detector-free probe in which a single frozen patch embedding re-identifies a lesion in a follow-up scan without registration, masks or fine-tuning. Because the conditioning is expressed through a spatial indicator rather than through label semantics, the formulation admits any weak spatial annotation; we instantiate and validate it for lesions.

</details>


### [104] [Robust and Efficient Motion Reasoning for Privacy-Aware Classroom Incident Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05115)
*Paritosh Parmar,Landy Lan,Hong Yang,Chen Yi,Chiat Pin Tay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种隐私感知、计算高效的教室事件识别框架。通过结合生成式CCTV风格视频与真实课堂姿态数据的混合基准，构建分层运动学表征，并利用知识蒸馏将大型教师模型的多阶运动推理能力压缩为小型单阶学生模型，在不到十分之一计算成本下超越大型基线模型，同时具备更强的跨域运动推理和零样本合成到真实泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教室安全是重要社会议题，但基于CCTV的教室事件识别领域尚未被充分探索：缺乏专门基准，也缺少针对隐私保护、计算效率和泛化能力需求设计的识别方法。现有方法难以满足真实部署中对隐私、效率和泛化性的综合要求。

Method: 1) 构建混合基准：结合生成式CCTV风格视频与真实课堂姿态数据。2) 提出轻量级但鲁棒的运动推理框架，核心观察是许多教室事件在运动方向、速度、加速度和强度上的差异比姿态本身更显著。3) 构建人类行为的分层运动学表征。4) 利用知识蒸馏，将大型教师模型中的分层多阶运动推理能力压缩为小型单阶学生模型，实现高效的逐人推理同时保留表达性运动理解。

Result: 实验表明，该模型以不到大型基线十分之一的计算成本取得超越性表现，并展现出更强的跨域运动推理能力和零样本合成到真实的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文验证了运动推理在教室事件识别中的有效性，证明了通过知识蒸馏实现轻量级高效模型的可能性，并将公开基准、代码库及支持工具，以促进隐私感知教室安全领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Can computer vision help make classrooms safer? In this pilot study, we investigate privacy-aware and computationally efficient classroom incident recognition from CCTV-style observations. This setting remains underexplored, with limited benchmarks and few methods designed for the privacy, efficiency, and generalization demands of real-world deployment. We introduce a novel hybrid benchmark combining generative CCTV-style videos with real-world classroom pose data, and propose a lightweight, but robust motion-reasoning framework motivated by the observation that many incidents differ more in motion direction, speed, acceleration, and intensity than in pose alone. To that end, our method first constructs hierarchical kinematic representations of human actions. Our method then distills hierarchical, multi-order kinematic reasoning from a large teacher into a much smaller single-order student, enabling efficient per-person inference while preserving expressive motion understanding. Experiments show that our model outperforms substantially larger baselines at less than one-tenth of their computational cost, while also demonstrating stronger out-of-domain motion reasoning and zero-shot synthetic-to-real generalization. We will publicly release the benchmark, codebase, and supporting tools to facilitate further research in privacy-aware classroom safety.

</details>


### [105] [HexMIL: Hierarchical Attention MIL for Ante-Hoc Explainable Detection of AI-Manipulated CT Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05101)
*Orazio Pontorno,Luca Guarnera,Zahid Akhtar,Sebastiano Battiato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出HexMIL，一种基于层级可解释多示例学习（MIL）的无掩码医学深度伪造检测器。它仅利用二值体积级标签，通过两层级patch/slice层次结构和独立门控注意力模块聚合，将注意力权重直接组合成全分辨率3D注意力体积，实现操控区域的定位与分类，克服了现有检测器泛化性差和缺乏可解释性的两大局限，在跨生成器泛化基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学深度伪造（由深度生成模型操控的医学图像）对临床工作流构成严重威胁，但现有检测器存在两个关键缺陷：一是对未见过的生成架构泛化能力差，二是缺乏可解释性。因此需要同时解决这两大问题。

Method: HexMIL将每个CT体积分解为patch和slice两层层次结构，通过独立的门控注意力（Gated Attention）模块进行聚合；各模块的注意力权重被直接组合成全分辨率3D注意力体积，用于定位被操控的子区域，全程无需任何像素级标注。与Grad-CAM等事后方法不同，HexMIL的注意力权重正是驱动分类决策的前向计算本身，提供事前（ante-hoc）且结构保真的空间归因。

Result: 在M3DSynth和CT-GAN数据集上，采用严格的跨生成器泛化协议（单一生成架构训练、不可见架构测试），HexMIL在域外分类任务中超越所有基线，AUC提升+9.1，F1提升+9.4；在定位任务中取得最佳平均IoU和Pointing Game得分。

Conclusion: HexMIL通过纯体积级监督同时解决了医学深度伪造检测中的泛化性与可解释性两大挑战，在跨生成器分类和篡改定位上均达到最优性能，为医学影像伪造检测提供了可靠且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: The emergence of medical deepfakes, i.e., medical images manipulated by deep generative models, poses a significant threat to clinical workflows. However, existing detectors suffer from two critical limitations: poor generalization to unseen generative architectures for manipulation detection and lack of interpretability. In this context, we present HexMIL (Hierarchical EXplainable Multiple Instance Learning), a mask-free medical deepfake detector that simultaneously addresses both limitations using only binary volume-level supervision. HexMIL decomposes each CT volume into a two-level hierarchy of patches and slices, aggregated via independent Gated Attention modules whose weights are directly combined into a full-resolution 3D attention volume that localizes the manipulated sub-region without any pixel-level annotation. Unlike post-hoc methods such as Grad-CAM, HexMIL's attention weights constitute the exact forward computation driving the classification decision, providing ante-hoc and structurally faithful spatial attribution. We evaluate HexMIL on M3DSynth and CT-GAN datasets under a rigorous cross-generator generalization protocol, training on a single generative architecture and testing on unseen ones. HexMIL outperforms all baselines by $+9.1$ AUC and $+9.4$ F1 in out-of-domain classification, and achieves the best average IoU and Pointing Game score in localization. Project page: opontorno.github.io/hexmil.

</details>


### [106] [Predicting Brain Morphometry with MT-GNN: Mesh Evolution in Continuous Time with Graph-Based Metric Tensor Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05132)
*Hao Ding,Daniel Semchin,Paul M. Thompson,Boris Gutman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-GNN，一种基于图神经网络的连续时间网格演化预测模型。它直接预测表面的内在几何（逐顶点第一基本形式/度量张量），通过可微的As-Rigid-As-Possible求解器解码为表面，并以端到端方式在刚性对齐的顶点误差上训练。在ADNI数据集14个皮质下结构上，MT-GNN在所有预测时间范围内均优于现有方法（DCM、TransforMesh），平均顶点误差较时间均值降低2.29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从几次先前的扫描中预测皮质下结构形状的演变，可用于预后支持和临床试验富集。现有方法要么通过高维嵌入外推形状轨迹，要么直接回归顶点变形，二者均未充分利用表面内在几何信息。作者希望预测连续时间中的表面内在几何，以实现任意时间范围的形状预测。

Method: MT-GNN采用单一图网络预测结构表面的逐顶点第一基本形式（度量张量）作为连续时间的函数。网络以任意因果多次访问历史和Fourier编码的领先时间（lead time）为条件。预测出的度量通过可微的As-Rigid-As-Possible（ARAP）求解器解码为表面网格，整个模型在刚性对齐的顶点误差上端到端训练。ARAP重建步骤保证了解码预测始终是有效表面，并提升了预测精度。

Result: 在ADNI数据集14个皮质下结构上，MT-GNN在所有预测时间范围均表现最佳。平均顶点误差相对时间均值降低2.29%（p=6.1×10⁻⁵），在14/14个结构上均优于时间均值；显著优于测地线形状回归DCM（-0.19%）和网格Transformer TransforMesh（-0.45%，p=1.2×10⁻⁴）。且随预测时间范围增大，优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 通过预测表面内在几何（度量张量）而非直接输出顶点坐标，并结合可微ARAP求解器进行端到端训练，MT-GNN在皮质下结构形状演变预测任务上取得了显著的性能提升，且优势随时间范围增大而扩大，验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Predicting how a subcortical structure's shape will evolve from a few prior scans could support prognosis and clinical-trial enrichment. Existing longitudinal mesh predictors either extrapolate shape trajectories via high-dimensional embeddings or regress vertex deformations directly. We instead predict the surface's intrinsic geometry in continuous time: a single per-structure graph network predicts the future per-vertex first fundamental form (metric tensor) for an arbitrary causal multiple-visit history and an arbitrary prediction horizon, conditioned on a Fourier encoding of the lead time. The predicted metric is decoded into a surface by a differentiable As-Rigid-As-Possible solver, and the model is trained end-to-end on the rigid-aligned vertex error. Training through the reconstruction keeps the decoded prediction a valid surface and consistently improves it. On 14 subcortical structures from the ADNI dataset, the proposed mesh evolution model (MT-GNN) predicts best among the evaluated methods at every horizon ($-2.29\%$ mean vertex error vs. the temporal mean, $p{=}6.1{\times}10^{-5}$, beating it on 14/14 structures), ahead of geodesic shape regression (DCM, $-0.19\%$) and a mesh transformer (TransforMesh, $-0.45\%$; $p{=}1.2{\times}10^{-4}$), with the lead widening as the horizon grows.

</details>


### [107] [IRIS: A Visual Cortex-Inspired Framework for Analyzing Orientation Selectivity in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05122)
*Vaishnavi B Mohan,Vijayakrishna Naganoor,Yashas Annadani,Shashank Hegde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉Transformer（ViT）中方向选择性这一生物基础特征是如何涌现的，提出了一套神经科学启发的度量指标（RSS、ORS、方向调谐带宽），发现训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，并展示了这些度量可作为下游微调层数选择的机制启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT缺乏归纳偏置，其机制上如何编码低层特征尚不清楚。生物视觉系统通过局部感受野构建方向选择性等低层特征，这些特征是多个专门神经通路共用的通用表示。作者想探究这些生物基础特征是否以及如何在ViT中涌现。

Method: 提出一套神经科学启发的度量指标：(1) 表示相似性分数（RSS）评估表示几何中方向的编码程度；(2) 方向招募分数（ORS）量化方向选择性单元的数量；(3) 方向调谐带宽衡量选择性单元的调谐锐度。通过深度维度系统分析这些指标在ViT训练过程中的变化。

Result: （1）训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，共享目标函数的模型在相对深度上峰值位置一致，与模型规模无关；（2）训练早期就有许多单元表现出方向选择性，早期到中间层随时间招募更多方向选择性单元，而深层则丧失选择性并拓宽调谐以转向语义编码；（3）这些度量可作为决定微调时应解冻多少层的机制启发式提示。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种在ViT训练过程中追踪生物基础特征的方法，揭示了期望属性如何在Transformer表示中被编码，并建立了对ViT跨任务泛化能力的系统性理解。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) have become the de facto standard for image encoding across many perception tasks. Despite their empirical success, it remains mechanistically unclear how they encode low-level features, given their lack of inductive biases: ViTs process information globally rather than relying on local structure. Biological visual systems, in contrast, build low-level features, such as orientation selectivity in the primary visual cortex, by combining information from small, localized regions of the visual field. These features are general-purpose representations, shared and required across multiple specialized neural pathways, unlike higher-level, task-specific semantic features. This raises the question if such biologically-grounded features arise in ViTs. In this work, we systematically study how orientation selectivity emerges in ViTs by introducing a suite of neuroscience-inspired metrics: representational similarity score (RSS), orientation recruitment score (ORS), and orientation tuning bandwidth to quantify how orientation is encoded in representational geometry and as a function of model depth. Through extensive analysis, we find that: (1) the training paradigm is the strongest determinant of orientation selectivity, with models sharing an objective, peaking at comparable relative depths regardless of scale (2) many units are orientation-selective early in training, with early-to-middle layers recruiting more such units over time, while deeper layers lose selectivity and broaden their tuning toward semantic encoding and (3) our metrics offer a mechanistic heuristic for how many layers to unfreeze for best downstream generalization. Our framework presents a way to track biologically-grounded features during ViT training, probes how desired properties are encoded in transformer representations, and builds a systematic understanding of how ViTs generalize across tasks.

</details>


### [108] [OPD-V: Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation with Modality Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05131)
*Aniri,Jinhe Bi,Peng Liao,Zengjie Jin,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPD-V，一种视觉自我蒸馏范式，通过构建正负教师（缩放图像与掩码图像）来利用模态平衡作为特权信息，解决多模态大语言模型中模态失衡导致的自我蒸馏受限问题，在6个基准、4个MLLM骨干和5种后训练方法上一致提升推理性能并降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的同策略自我蒸馏（OPSD）方法利用多样输入源提供的特权信息来引导蒸馏，但忽视了多模态大语言模型推理中固有的模态失衡（Modality Imbalance）问题。当文本信息主导生成时，模型无法充分整合多模态输入，导致精心设计的特权信息未被充分利用，限制了OPSD的效果。

Method: OPD-V通过构建正教师（缩放图像）和负教师（掩码图像）来体现不同程度的模态失衡，将这三种教师间的推理正确性与token logits差异作为特权信息。具体而言，利用正负教师的模态平衡logits边际定义模态平衡信任区域（Modality-Balance Trust Region），据此选择用于自我蒸馏的同策略token。

Result: 在6个基准、4种MLLM骨干和5种后训练方法的广泛实验中，OPD-V一致地提升了推理性能，同时减少了训练成本。

Conclusion: 模态失衡是多模态推理中同策略自我蒸馏效果受限的关键因素，而模态平衡本身可以作为特权信息来指导自我蒸馏。OPD-V通过模态平衡信任区域选择token，有效提升了多模态推理性能并降低了训练开销。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has become a standard post-training approach for improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing methods draw privileged information from diverse input sources to guide self-distillation. Yet these designs overlook Modality Imbalance, a challenge inherent to MLLM reasoning. When textual information dominates generation, the model cannot fully integrate its multimodal input. Consequently, carefully designed privileged information remains underused, limiting the effectiveness of OPSD. To examine this limitation, we construct a Positive Teacher with the Zoom-In Image and a Negative Teacher with the Mask Image, which exhibit different degrees of Modality Imbalance. Changes in their reasoning correctness and token logits reveal that Modality Balance can itself serve as privileged information. Motivated by this finding, we introduce OPD-V, a visual OPSD paradigm that instantiates such information through the Positive Teacher and Negative Teacher. Positive Modality-Balance Logits Margins define a Modality-Balance Trust Region that selects the on-policy tokens used for self-distillation. Experiments across 6 benchmarks, 4 MLLM backbones, and 5 post-training methods show that OPD-V consistently improves reasoning performance while reducing training cost.

</details>


### [109] [SmartMage: Dynamic Modality Orchestration for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05137)
*Yue Zhang,Yingzhao Jian,Yunqiu Xu,Xiaoxiao Sun,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartMage是一个统一的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），通过动态编排异构模态（视觉与几何信息）进行语义感知的3D场景理解，解决现有MLLM依赖固定模态组合导致的语义噪声与推理效率低下的问题。该模型包含两个核心模块：语义引导的模态自适应路由（SMART）模块和模态感知门控专家（MAGE）模块，在五个3D场景理解基准上取得SOTA性能，并在RGB视频理解基准上获得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景理解需要联合推理来自多模态的异构信息（视觉和几何线索），但不同查询对这些模态的相关性需求各异。现有MLLM通常依赖固定模态组合，忽略了查询对模态的依赖性需求，这种刚性设计会引入不相关模态的语义噪声，同时未能充分利用更有信息量的模态，导致计算浪费和推理能力被稀释。

Method: SmartMage提出两个关键模块：(1) 语义引导的模态自适应路由（SMART）模块，利用语义先验、文本-模态对齐和模态质量来动态选择任务相关的模态；(2) 模态感知门控专家（MAGE）模块，利用模态先验引导专家激活，促进多模态推理中的自适应专业化。论文还构建了细粒度语义类别的诊断基准ScanFacet，用于分析不同语义类型偏好的模态组合。

Result: SmartMage在五个3D场景理解基准上取得SOTA性能，在RGB-only视频理解基准上获得有竞争力的结果。诊断基准ScanFacet中观测到的模态-语义模式进一步佐证了SmartMage的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态模态编排对于3D场景理解中的多模态推理至关重要。通过语义感知的模态选择与门控专家激活，SmartMage能够有效避免固定模态组合带来的语义噪声和计算浪费，在多个基准上验证了该方法的有效性，并揭示了不同语义类型对模态的偏好模式。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes is fundamental to embodied intelligence, requiring joint reasoning over heterogeneous information from multiple modalities, including visual and geometric cues. However, the relevance of these modalities often varies across queries. Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically rely on fixed modality combinations, overlooking query-dependent modality needs. Such a rigid design can introduce semantic noise from irrelevant modalities while underutilizing more informative ones, leading to wasted computation and diluted reasoning. To address these challenges, this paper proposes SmartMage, a unified MLLM that dynamically orchestrates heterogeneous modalities for semantic-aware 3D scene understanding. Specifically, SmartMage incorporates: (1) a Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive RouTng (SMART) module that selects task-relevant modalities using semantic priors, text-modality alignment, and modality quality; and (2) a Modality-Aware Gating Expert (MAGE) module that leverages modality priors to guide expert activation, fostering adaptive specialization in multimodal reasoning. Empirically, SmartMage achieves state-of-the-art performance across five 3D scene understanding benchmarks, and attains competitive results on RGB-only video understanding benchmarks. In our diagnostic benchmark ScanFacet, tasks are divided into fine-grained semantic categories, enabling analysis of modality combinations preferred by each semantic type. The observed modality-semantic patterns provide further evidence of SmartMage's effectiveness. Project page: https://yuecheong.github.io/SmartMage/.

</details>


### [110] [Objects as Audio-Visual Modal Sound Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05145)
*Zisen Shao,Zihao Wei,Derong Jin,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Audio-Visual Modal Sound Field (AV-MSF)，一种从多视角图像和少量碰撞音记录重建物体级声场的新型表征。该方法将3D高斯泼溅与稠密3D视觉特征结合，为声学重建提供几何感知先验，并使用紧凑且物理有意义的模态参数来表示碰撞声场，实现少样本鲁棒重建。在真实数据集上达到SOTA碰撞声渲染效果，并支持接触定位和物体声音编辑等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代3D重建擅长建模物体几何和外观，却忽略了物理交互中丰富的声学线索。碰撞声蕴含材料、刚度和结构属性，可补充视觉信息；然而现有碰撞声建模方法要么依赖昂贵的物理仿真，要么需要大规模数据才能实现纯数据驱动的泛化。

Method: AV-MSF基于3D高斯泼溅框架，融合稠密3D视觉特征以提供强几何感知先验，将碰撞声场表示为紧凑且物理意义明确的模态参数，在少量碰撞声样本条件下实现少样本物体级声学重建。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，AV-MSF在碰撞声渲染方面达到最先进水平，优于基于物理仿真和纯数据驱动的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该表示不仅实现了高质量的碰撞声渲染，还支持接触定位和物体声音编辑等下游应用，展示了视觉-声学联合表征的广阔潜力。

Abstract: While modern 3D reconstruction excels at modeling object geometry and appearance, it largely ignores the rich acoustic cues revealed through physical interaction. Object impact sounds convey material, stiffness, and structural properties that complement vision, yet existing impact sound modeling approaches either rely on expensive physics-based simulation or require large datasets to generalize in a purely data-driven manner. We introduce Audio-Visual Modal Sound Field (AV-MSF), a novel object-level acoustic representation reconstructed from multi-view images and only a few impact sound recordings. AV-MSF builds on 3D Gaussian Splatting integrated with dense 3D visual feature to provide a strong geometry-aware prior, and represents the impact sound field using compact, physically meaningful modal parameters, enabling robust few-shot reconstruction. Experiments on two real-world datasets show that AV-MSF achieves state-of-the-art impact sound rendering, outperforming both physics-based and data-driven baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate downstream applications enabled by our representation, including contact localization and object sound editing.

</details>


### [111] [CoCo-IR: Contextual Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05149)
*Shengcao Cao,Tanmaya Shekhar Dabral,Zhongli Ding,Madhuri Shanbhogue,Kaifeng Chen,Zhe Li,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo-IR（上下文组合图像检索）任务，允许用户通过多轮交互逐步细化搜索结果。作者基于大型多模态模型（LMM）设计了上下文感知推理器，生成可跨轮次演化的可变换图像嵌入（TIE），并构建了无需人工标注的全自动可扩展数据引擎。在CIRCO基准上达到39.4 mAP@5，在CoCo-IR基准上4轮对话达到44.1 R@1，远超现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于指令的图像检索系统虽然强大，但仅限于单轮交互，无法捕捉复杂现实视觉搜索中固有的迭代性。用户在实际搜索过程中往往需要逐步细化需求，现有方法无法利用对话历史来持续优化检索结果。

Method: 1) 提出新型任务CoCo-IR，支持用户通过多轮交互逐步细化图像检索；2) 基于大型多模态模型（LMM）构建上下文感知推理器，理解整个交互历史并生成可跨轮次演化的可变换图像嵌入（TIE）；3) 开发全自动可扩展数据引擎，利用LMM生成高质量上下文检索数据，并通过模型引导验证挖掘困难负样本。

Result: 在单轮基准CIRCO上达到39.4 mAP@5（SOTA）；在自建的CoCo-IR基准上，4轮对话达到44.1 R@1，大幅超越现有方法（28.2 R@1）。

Conclusion: CoCo-IR成功填补了多轮上下文图像检索的空白，数据引擎方案有效避免了昂贵的人工标注成本，为未来迭代式视觉搜索系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current instruction-based image retrieval systems are powerful but limited to single-turn interactions, failing to capture the iterative nature of complex, real-world visual searches. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Contextual Composed Image Retrieval (CoCo-IR), a novel task that enables users to progressively refine search results through interactions. We address this new task by proposing a new model based on a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) that functions as a context-aware reasoner for CoCo-IR. Our model interprets the entire interaction history to generate Transformable Image Embeddings (TIE) that evolve across turns. To fuel the model training without expensive human annotations, we develop a fully autonomous, scalable data engine that leverages LMMs to generate high-quality contextual retrieval data, and uses model-guided verification to mine challenging hard negatives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach establishes new state-of-the-art performance: We achieve 39.4 mAP@5 on the challenging single-turn benchmark CIRCO; furthermore, on our new CoCo-IR benchmark, our model maintains robust performance with 44.1 R@1 on 4-turn dialogues, dramatically outperforming existing methods (28.2 4-turn R@1) that fail to handle multi-turn context. Project page: https://CoCo-IR.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Hardware-Enabled Fuzzy Inference: Architectures, Platforms, and Emerging Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04031)
*Amir Hossein Jalilvand,Parsa Hassani Shariat Panahi,M. Hassan Najafi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文献是首个以平台为中心的硬件模糊系统综述，系统性地将相关文献分为FPGA实现、ASIC/定制VLSI实现以及嵌入式/IoT/TinyML平台三大类，并进行了跨平台对比分析，指出当前研究空白并展望未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件模糊推理在实时边缘智能场景下存在延迟不可预测、功耗过高和资源利用效率低等局限，亟需硬件加速方案，但目前缺乏一个系统化、平台视角的综述来整合这一领域的碎片化研究。

Method: 采用平台中心化分类方法，将文献归纳为FPGA、ASIC/定制VLSI和嵌入式/IoT/TinyML三类，分别从架构组织、资源映射策略、实现权衡和设计挑战等维度进行分析，并进行跨平台比较。

Result: 跨平台对比显示：FPGA提供灵活性和快速原型能力，ASIC实现峰值性能和能效，嵌入式/TinyML系统在低功耗和成本方面平衡最佳。同时识别出标准化基准缺失、规则库扩展性有限、设计自动化不足、在线学习和新兴存储技术支持不足等关键研究空白。

Conclusion: 指出未来研究方向包括基于忆阻器交叉阵列的内存模糊计算、与TinyML生态的整合、可解释硬件AI以及开源设计自动化，为硬件模糊智能领域的研究者与实践者提供参考框架。

Abstract: Fuzzy logic systems are widely used for intelligent decision-making under uncertainty, offering interpretability and robustness across diverse applications. However, the growing demand for real-time edge intelligence has exposed the limitations of software-based fuzzy inference: unpredictable latency, excessive power consumption, and inefficient resource utilization. This has motivated extensive research into hardware acceleration, spanning platforms from custom analog circuits and digital ASICs to reconfigurable FPGAs and ultra-low-power microcontrollers. This survey presents the first comprehensive, platform-centric review of hardware fuzzy systems, systematically organizing the literature into three principal categories: FPGA-based implementations, ASIC and custom VLSI realizations, and embedded, IoT, and TinyML platforms. For each category, we analyze architectural organization, resource mapping strategies, implementation trade-offs, and key design challenges.
  Our cross-platform comparative analysis reveals that no single platform dominates across all metrics. FPGAs offer flexibility and rapid prototyping, ASICs deliver peak performance and energy efficiency, while embedded and TinyML systems balance low power and cost for edge deployment. Despite significant progress, critical research gaps persist: the absence of standardized benchmarks, limited scalability of rule bases, insufficient design automation, and limited support for online learning and emerging memory technologies. We outline future directions including in-memory fuzzy computing with memristive crossbars, integration with TinyML ecosystems, explainable hardware AI, and open-source design automation. This survey serves as a {reference for} researchers and practitioners working on hardware-enabled fuzzy intelligence.

</details>


### [113] [EDATracer: An Agentic Framework for Large-Scale EDA Artifact Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04032)
*Phat Tieu,Sayanti Jana,Matthew DeLorenzo,Jiawen Wu,Narendran Srinivasan,Srinivas Shakkottai,Jiang Hu,Jeyavijayan Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出EDATracer，一个用于芯片设计EDA工件分析的智能体框架。它将设计工件组织为知识图谱与语义向量索引，使LLM智能体能在源文件、日志、网表和报告中检索证据。提供了一个18.9GB包含2,787个可综合开源芯片设计的数据集和90个问题的基准。相比Cursor和Claude Code，在pass@1准确率上平均提升6.4和7.2个百分点，同时节省2.0-3.2倍token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代芯片设计依赖EDA工具生成大量异构工件（源文件、脚本、日志、网表、报告），跨工件和跨设计阶段的分析对调试、优化和流程理解至关重要，但现有LLM智能体缺乏大规模跨工件分析公共基准，且难以在工具生成的证据上进行推理接地。

Method: EDATracer将设计工件组织为知识图谱与语义向量索引的联合结构，使LLM智能体能够在源文件、日志、网表和报告之间检索证据；同时构建了一个包含2,787个可综合开源芯片设计的18.9GB数据集和90个问题（涵盖事实性、统计性和推理性任务）的基准。

Result: 在评估的智能体中，EDATracer取得最佳pass@1准确率，平均比Cursor高6.4个百分点、比Claude Code高7.2个百分点，同时使用2.0-3.2倍更少的token。

Conclusion: EDATracer通过将工具生成的证据结构化为可检索的知识图谱和语义索引，显著提升了LLM智能体在EDA工件交叉分析上的准确性和效率，填补了该领域缺乏公共基准的空白。

Abstract: Modern chip design relies on electronic design automation (EDA) tools that generate large, heterogeneous artifacts, including source files, scripts, logs, netlists, and reports. Analyzing these artifacts is critical for debugging, optimization, and design-flow understanding, but remains difficult because relevant evidence is often distributed across many artifact types and design stages. Although LLM agents show promise for EDA assistance, existing approaches lack public benchmarks for large-scale cross-artifact analysis and often struggle to ground reasoning in tool-generated evidence. We present EDATracer, an agentic framework for evidence-grounded EDA artifact analysis. EDATracer organizes design artifacts into a knowledge graph paired with a semantic vector index, enabling LLM agents to retrieve evidence across source files, logs, netlists, and reports. We curate an 18.9 GB dataset of 2,787 synthesizable open-source chip designs and introduce a 90-question benchmark spanning factual, statistical, and reasoning tasks. Across evaluated agents, EDATracer achieves the best pass@1 accuracy, outperforming Cursor and Claude Code by 6.4 and 7.2 percentage points on average, while using 2.0-3.2x fewer tokens.

</details>


### [114] [CheckOne: Lightweight Fault Detection and Mitigation for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04035)
*Mohammad Hasan Ahmadilivani,Sven-Markus Loorits,Jaan Raik*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出CheckOne，一种面向Vision Transformer（ViT）的轻量级算法级容错（ABFT）方法，显著降低了计算开销，在多个ViT模型上实现了高达26倍的故障缓解效果和平均3.8倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在安全关键应用中的广泛部署引发了硬件故障相关的可靠性担忧。现有ABFT方法虽然为DNN提供了轻量级对称保护，但由于ViT巨大的计算需求，传统ABFT方法难以有效应用于ViT，亟需一种针对ViT特性的低成本故障检测与缓解方案。

Method: 本文首先全面评估了ViT的可靠性，强调其各层需要对称保护的必要性。在此基础上提出了CheckOne方法，这是一种新颖且成本有效的ViT故障检测与缓解方案，通过显著降低计算开销的方式超越传统ABFT方法在ViT上的表现。

Result: 在多个ViT模型上进行的大量实验表明，CheckOne能够将关键故障缓解率提升高达26倍，并在ViT上实现比传统ABFT平均高3.8倍的性能。

Conclusion: CheckOne是一种适用于ViT的高效、低成本可靠性保护方案，在显著降低计算开销的同时实现了优于传统ABFT的故障检测与缓解能力，为ViT在安全关键领域的可靠部署提供了有力支持。

Abstract: The wide adoption of Vision Transformers (ViTs) in safety-critical applications raises reliability concerns related to hardware faults. Algorithm-Based Fault Tolerance (ABFT) methods have emerged as lightweight and symmetric protection mechanisms for DNNs. However, they are particularly challenging for ViTs due to their significant computational requirements. This work comprehensively evaluates the reliability of ViTs, emphasizing the need for symmetric protection in their layers. Furthermore, we present CheckOne, a novel, cost-effective method for fault detection and mitigation in ViTs that significantly reduces the computational cost compared to conventional ABFT. Through extensive experiments with multiple ViTs, CheckOne mitigates critical faults by up to $26\times$ and achieves an average 3.8x higher performance than ABFT in ViTs.

</details>


### [115] [A Symbolic Execution Framework for Symbolic Timing Analysis of Digital Integrated Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04036)
*Dennis Eigner,Arman Ferdowsi,Ulrich Schmid*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于符号执行的新型数字集成电路动态时序分析框架，利用先进的分析型门延迟模型（能够捕捉drafting和多输入切换等效应），为电路中所有路径自动计算符号延迟表达式，并通过符号剪枝方法缓解组合爆炸问题，实现路径敏感、目标驱动的时序推理和电路路径的解析优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于仿真的动态时序分析（DDTA）虽然比SPICE仿真快，但其精度依赖于超越纯延迟或惯性延迟模型的先进门延迟模型。现有仿真方法无法穷尽地探索所有时序场景，需要一种能够利用先进解析延迟模型进行更全面、更具表达力的时序分析方法。

Method: 提出一个符号执行框架的基石，利用解析门延迟模型（涵盖drafting和多输入切换效应），在给定输入跳变顺序的条件下，为数字电路中所有路径自动计算符号延迟表达式；同时引入符号剪枝方法以减少组合爆炸，支持路径敏感和目标驱动的时序推理以及特定电路路径的解析优化。

Result: 该框架能够自动生成所有路径的符号延迟表达式，并通过符号剪枝技术有效控制组合复杂度，支持路径敏感、目标驱动的时序属性推理和电路路径的解析化优化。

Conclusion: 基于符号执行与先进解析延迟模型的结合，为数字电路时序分析提供了超越传统仿真方法的新范式，有望实现更精确、更全面且具有推理能力的时序验证与优化。

Abstract: Simulation-based dynamic timing analysis of digital integrated circuits (DDTA) offers a faster alternative to traditional analog SPICE simulations. To achieve timing predictions that are reasonably competitive in terms of accuracy, however, DDTA mandates gate delay models that go beyond the standard pure or inertial delay models used in state-of-the-art tools. Recent advances in analytic gate delay models, which now also capture effects like drafting and multi-input switching, unlock new possibilities for timing analysis, which go way beyond simulation-based approaches towards an exhaustive exploration. In this paper, we present the cornerstones of a novel symbolic execution framework, which utilizes such analytic delay models for automatically computing symbolic delay expressions for all paths in a digital circuit, for some given ordering of the input transitions. To reduce combinatorial explosion, we introduce symbolic pruning methods that also enable path-sensitive, goal-driven reasoning about timing properties and analytic optimization of specific circuit paths.

</details>


### [116] [On Design Principles for Efficient Heterogeneous DRAM-PIM-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04169)
*Corey Lammie,Hadjer Benmeziane,William Andrew Simon,Irem Boybat*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了异构DRAM-PIM-GPU系统在LLM解码阶段推理中的性能，揭示了三个关键设计原则：静态功耗（DRAM泄漏、刷新、GPU空闲功耗）可主导效率计算，动态功耗模型会高估tokens/s/W达3.85倍；解码性能随通道数单调非递减且在低批量时趋于饱和，且所有模型在固定容量扫描下共享近似最优层级配置；工作负载映射策略带来的提升有限，需要系统级协同优化才能实现显著效率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构DRAM-PIM-GPU系统设计实践忽略了决定实际性能的关键因素（如静态功耗、通道配置、工作负载映射），导致对解码阶段LLM推理效率的评估不够准确，无法为下一代内存加速LLM系统提供可靠的设计指导。

Method: 通过对多样化架构（OPT-7B/70B自回归模型和Mamba2-2.7B/70B状态空间模型）与工作负载进行系统化评估，比较在不同批量大小、输入/输出token长度下的性能，分析静态功耗、通道数扫描、固定容量层级配置以及工作负载映射策略对效率的影响。

Result: 发现静态功耗可导致动态功耗模型高估tokens/s/W达3.85倍；解码性能随通道数单调非递减并在高通道数时趋于饱和；固定容量下所有模型共享近似最优层级配置但注意力模型对配置错误惩罚更大；工作负载映射仅提供至多14.0%/17.4%的kernel级延迟/能耗降低和5.6%的端到端增益。

Conclusion: 显著的效率提升需要系统级协同优化（静态功耗管理+通道配置+层级配置+工作负载映射的联合设计），而非单点优化；这些设计原则为架构师设计下一代内存加速LLM系统提供了设计空间指南。

Abstract: Heterogeneous DRAM-based processing-in-memory (PIM)-GPU systems promise significant efficiency gains for decode-phase large language model (LLM) inference, particularly in long-output generation, yet current design practices overlook critical factors that determine real-world performance. Through systematic evaluation of diverse architectures and workloads (OPT-7B/70B, Mamba2-2.7B/70B), we reveal three fundamental design principles: (i) static power consumption (DRAM leakage, refresh, and GPU idle power) can dominate the efficiency calculus, causing dynamic-only models to overestimate tokens/s/W by up to 3.85X for realistic deployments (Mamba2-2.7B, batch size 1, 128 input tokens, and 2,048 output tokens); (ii) decoding performance is monotonically non-decreasing with channel count across all evaluated models and workloads, generally plateauing at high channel counts for low-batch workloads; under a fixed-capacity sweep, all models instead share a common near-optimal hierarchy configuration, with substantially larger misconfiguration penalties for attention-based models; (iii) workload mapping strategies provide bounded improvements (up to 14.0%/17.4% kernel-level latency/energy reduction, up to 5.6% end-to-end gain) and are not primary bottlenecks. Significant efficiency gains require system-wide co-optimization. These principles provide design-space guidance for architects designing the next generation of memory-accelerated LLM systems.

</details>


### [117] [A Centralized Performance Monitoring Architecture for Heterogeneous Multicore SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04247)
*Mohammed Sajjad Jafri,Abdur Rahman,Emon Sarkar,Mahdi Hassen,Gopishankar Thayyil,Ashwin Krishna Mani,Rodolfo Pellizzoni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向嵌入式SoC的集中式性能监控架构，通过Event Monitoring Units (EVUs)捕获微架构事件并转发至Advanced Performance Monitoring Unit (APMU)，APMU集成可编程计数器和专用处理单元，支持跨组件的性能事件收集、关联和处理。该设计已在AXI4-based系统和RISC-V SoC平台上实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代嵌入式SoC中，不同组件（处理器、加速器、互连、内存控制器）各自实现了独立且异构的硬件性能计数器（HPCs），这种分布式监控基础设施需要多个软件接口，导致性能数据的收集、同步和关联变得复杂。因此需要一种集中式监控架构来简化跨组件性能事件的管理。

Method: 提出集中式性能监控架构：1) 设计Event Monitoring Units (EVUs)用于捕获并转发微架构事件；2) 构建Advanced Performance Monitoring Unit (APMU)集成可编程计数器和专用处理单元，支持灵活的事件驱动软件机制；3) 在AXI4-based系统和RISC-V SoC平台上实现该设计。

Result: 通过实时资源调节、应用性能分析和计数器归属三个案例研究，验证了该集中式监控架构在跨组件性能数据收集、关联和处理方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的集中式性能监控架构能够有效解决分布式HPC模块带来的数据收集和关联复杂性问题，为缺乏高级跨组件性能监控支持的RISC-V SoC平台提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Hardware Performance Counters (HPCs) are widely used to enable event-driven software mechanisms such as profile guided optimization, performance analysis, and dynamic resource management in real-time systems. However, in modern embedded System-on-a-Chip (SoCs), different components - including processors, accelerators, interconnects, and memory controllers - typically implement separate and heterogeneous HPC modules. This distributed monitoring infrastructure requires multiple software interfaces and complicates the collection, synchronization, and correlation of performance data across the system. In this work, we propose a centralized performance monitoring architecture to efficiently collect, correlate, and process architectural events across multiple hardware components. Our design introduces Event Monitoring Units (EVUs) that capture and forward microarchitectural events to an Advanced Performance Monitoring Unit (APMU). The APMU integrates programmable counters and a specialized processing element to support flexible, event-driven software mechanisms. We implement our design for an AXI4-based system and integrate it into a RISC-V based SoC platform, which lacks advanced cross-component performance monitoring support. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through case studies on real-time resource regulation, application profiling, and counter attribution.

</details>


### [118] [Deltoris: Enabling Real-time VLA Inference in Embodied AI via Bit-level Sparsity and Speculative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04428)
*Zheng Liu,Zeyu Guo,Zihan Liu,Anbang Wu,Han Zhao,Fangxin Liu,Zhezhi He,Yinhe Han,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo,Yiming Gan,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Deltoris是一个面向扩散式视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过时间感知的比特稀疏算法、推测推理技术以及定制化的1D脉动比特串行PE阵列加速器，在边缘设备上实现高效的VLA推理，相比移动GPU可获得最高34.2倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式VLA模型在具身智能中展现出优越的动作质量和泛化能力，但其计算密集且需在高控制频率（50-200 Hz）下运行，对边缘设备的延迟和能耗提出了严格要求，亟需高效的端侧推理方案。

Method: 1) 提出时间感知比特稀疏（temporal-aware bit-sparsity）算法：利用连续输入之间的时间相似性，仅计算相邻输入的差异部分，消除冗余的比特级运算。2) 提出推测推理（speculative inference）技术：将数据加载分摊到多个控制步骤中，以解决算法引入的额外片外访存开销。3) 协同设计专用加速器，采用定制的1D脉动比特串行PE阵列，消除PE工作负载不均衡问题。

Result: Deltoris相比移动GPU实现最高34.2倍加速，相比先前的加速器实现6.1倍加速，同时维持了可比的模型精度。

Conclusion: 通过算法层的时间冗余消除与硬件层的架构协同优化，Deltoris有效解决了扩散式VLA模型在边缘设备上的推理瓶颈，为具身智能的端侧部署提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as a key component in embodied AI. Among existing approaches, diffusion-based VLA models achieve superior motion quality and generalization. However, diffusion-based VLA models are compute-intensive and must run at high control frequency, e.g., 50-200 Hz. Thus, it imposes strict latency and energy constraints on edge devices.
  In this work, we present Deltoris, an algorithm-hardware co-design framework for efficient diffusion-based VLA inference. First, we exploit the temporal similarity of consecutive inputs and propose a \textit{temporal-aware bit-sparsity} algorithm that computes only the differences between consecutive inputs, eliminating redundant bit-level operations. To further address the extra off-chip traffic introduced by our algorithm, we propose a \textit{speculative inference} technique, which amortizes data loading across multiple control steps. Lastly, to support these techniques, we co-design a dedicated accelerator with customized 1D systolic bit-serial PE arrays that eliminate PE workload imbalance. Our evaluation shows that Deltoris achieves up to 34.2$\times$ speedup over mobile GPUs and 6.1$\times$ over prior accelerators, while maintaining comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [MCHA: A Memory-Centric Hierarchical Architecture for Parallel-Sequential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04443)
*Daijing Shi,Hongxiao Zhao,Yihan Fu,Zhan Chen,Jiayi Li,Yihang Zhu,Anjunyi Fan,Yaoyu Tao,Yuchao Yang,Bonan Yan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出MCHA（Memory-Centric Hierarchical Architecture），一种面向并行-顺序混合执行模式的可重构硬件架构，通过层次化通信策略和事件驱动的条件触发编程模型，显著降低主存带宽压力，在MARL等新兴工作负载上相对NVIDIA A100 GPU实现153倍至2457倍的性能加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习（MARL）、大规模神经形态计算和概率图模型等新兴工作负载本质上是并行-顺序混合计算模式，既需要大规模并行以实现高吞吐，又受限于集中在主存的不规则数据访问模式。传统架构在执行这些负载时面临全局缓冲区饱和和内存带宽瓶颈的根本性限制。

Method: MCHA采用层次化通信策略，支持分布式核间数据路由，降低全局内存带宽负担；同时引入了一种基于事件驱动条件触发的新型并行-顺序编程模型，在流水线执行中隐藏数据传输延迟。MCHA通过28nm工艺综合实现，并配套开源周期精确模拟器进行验证。

Result: 在MARL工作负载上，相对NVIDIA A100 GPU实现153.06倍至2456.96倍的加速比；将主存访问比例从96%降至5.44%。28nm工艺下面积2.92mm²，功耗115.36mW（200MHz）。

Conclusion: MCHA通过内存中心的层次化硬件架构与配套编程模型，有效解决了并行-顺序工作负载中的内存瓶颈问题，在大幅提升性能的同时保持了跨应用领域的编程灵活性。

Abstract: Emerging workloads, such as Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), large-scale neuromorphic computing, and probabilistic graphical models, intrinsically exhibit parallel-sequential computing patterns. While these tasks demand massive parallelism to achieve high throughput, they are severely bottlenecked by irregular data access patterns centralized to main memory. Consequently, conventional architectures face fundamental limitations when executing these workloads, primarily manifesting as global buffer saturation and memory-bound bottlenecks. To address these challenges, we propose the Memory-Centric Hierarchical Architecture (MCHA), a reconfigurable hardware solution tailored for parallel-sequential execution. MCHA leverages a hierarchical communication strategy that facilitates distributed, inter-core data routing, thereby significantly reducing the bandwidth burden on the global memory. Complementing the hardware, MCHA introduces a novel parallel-sequential programming model that utilizes event-driven conditional triggers to effectively hide data transmission latency within the execution pipeline. We benchmark MCHA against a diverse suite of parallel-sequential tasks, including MARL, motor variable control, and Markov random fields. Validated through our open-source, cycle-accurate simulator, MCHA demonstrates performance speedups ranging from 153.06$\times$ to 2456.96$\times$ over NVIDIA A100 GPUs on MARL workloads, while maintaining robust programming flexibility across other application domains. Furthermore, the architecture successfully reduces main memory access from 96% to 5.44%. When synthesized in a 28 nm process, the MCHA implementation occupies an area footprint of 2.92mm$^2$ and consumes 115.36 mW of power at 200 MHz. MCHA is open-sourced at https://github.com/carabdis/MCHA.

</details>


### [120] [A Systolic Array Architecture for Nonlinear Activation Functions and Softmax Computation using Chebyshev Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04734)
*Benedikt Schaible,Anirudh Suresh Bharadwaj,Ulf Schlichtmann,Jiang Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于脉动阵列的激活单元架构，通过切比雪夫多项式逼近，在统一的硬件上同时支持多种单变量激活函数（如tanh）和多变量softmax函数。相比CORDIC基线，tanh的均方绝对误差降低了71%，面积和功耗分别减少4.6%和5.1%；softmax逼近相比CORDIC和分段线性逼近，KL散度分别降低了44.6%和79.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的硬件加速器通常为单变量激活函数（如tanh）和多变量softmax函数使用不同的硬件单元，导致无法在两者之间进行资源共享，造成硬件资源浪费。作者希望通过统一的架构设计来支持两类激活函数，以提高硬件利用率。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于脉动阵列的激活单元架构，通过切比雪夫（Chebyshev）多项式逼近来同时支持多种单变量激活函数以及softmax函数。该方法利用切比雪夫近似的良好数值特性，将各类激活函数的计算统一映射到脉动阵列硬件上。

Result: 在tanh函数上，相比CORDIC基线实现了71%更低的平均绝对误差，同时面积减少4.6%、功耗降低5.1%；在softmax函数上，相比CORDIC和分段线性逼近方案，KL散度分别降低了44.6%和79.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过精心设计的统一激活单元架构以及切比雪夫多项式逼近，可以在节省硬件面积和功耗的同时，显著提升激活函数的计算精度，为神经网络加速器的资源复用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Neural Network Accelerators have gained popularity in recent years due to their greater efficiency than CPU-based platforms. Often, these accelerators utilize different hardware units for univariate activation functions, such as tanh, and the multivariate softmax, thereby missing opportunities for resource sharing between them. In this paper, we describe a novel systolic array-based activation unit architecture that supports multiple univariate activation functions as well as the softmax function. By utilizing Chebyshev polynomial approximations, our activation function unit achieves up to 71% lower mean absolute error for tanh compared to a CORDIC baseline, while using 4.6% less area and 5.1% less power. Our softmax approximation enables a 44.6% and 79.0% lower KL divergence compared to CORDIC and a piecewise-linear approximation, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C2MOE框架，针对多模态对话情感识别中模态缺失问题，通过一致性与互补性引导的混合专家机制，在信息论框架下统一表示学习与缺失模态插补，在多个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MERC方法依赖完整多模态输入，但实际场景中因传输错误或用户行为导致模态缺失，严重降低模型性能。已有方法通过跨模态一致性学习增强鲁棒性，但忽略了模态互补性，导致插补结果存在偏差。

Method: C2MOE通过信息论框架将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性两部分：一致性通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕获，互补性通过最大化模态间条件熵保留。在此基础上引入双分支预测机制——一致性分支通过最小化不确定性对齐联合分布，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态独特线索。最后使用可学习的重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性分数，实现自适应融合插补。

Result: 在多个MERC基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C2MOE在各种缺失模态设置下持续超越最先进的基线方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: C2MOE通过同时建模模态一致性与互补性，并采用动态专家加权融合策略，有效解决了不完整多模态情感识别中的插补偏差问题，为鲁棒MERC提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [122] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为次优势（minimax）超度量算子建立了ℓ₀型（稀疏）稳定性理论。研究表明，稀疏编辑只通过最小生成树传播：超度量值仅在树路径穿越被编辑边或编辑的树外边暴露的新切割时改变。文章给出了逐编辑的暴露切割分数、仅依赖树的全局包络线以及Hamming-Lipschitz界，并证明了锐度结果（严格切割分离下界精确可达，单个编辑可改变Θ(n²)个超度量条目），还证明了多编辑的条件近可加性原理。实验表明结构分数可用于层次表示的脆弱性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的次优势超度量稳定性理论通常以ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff度量表述，这类界不适合只改变少数成对距离的稀疏扰动场景。稀疏编辑需要一种新的稳定性理论来刻画其影响范围。

Method: 作者发展了针对次优势超度量算子的ℓ₀型稳定性理论。核心思想是分析稀疏编辑如何通过最小生成树传播：推导了逐编辑的暴露切割分数、仅依赖树结构（tree-only）的全局包络线，建立了Hamming-Lipschitz界（即改变的超度量条目数量的上界）。同时构造了反例族证明结果的锐度，并证明多编辑情形下在认证大变化区域和可忽略重叠条件下的条件近可加性原理。

Result: 获得了稀疏编辑下超度量条目变化数目的精确界；证明了锐度结果：在严格切割分离条件下树边界的界可精确达到，而离树编辑族中单个编辑距离改变可导致Θ(n²)个超度量条目变动。实验基于深度嵌入图验证了结构分数作为层次表示脆弱性诊断工具的有效性。

Conclusion: ℓ₀型稳定性理论比传统的ℓ∞/Gromov-Hausdorff框架更适合刻画稀疏扰动下的次优势超度量行为，揭示了其稳定性本质上由树几何（MST结构）支配，且该依赖性不可回避。该理论为层次表示的脆弱性诊断提供了实用的结构性分数。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [123] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信任域框架来解释Adam等自适应矩估计机制在随机梯度优化中的行为，该框架通过p阶（p∈[2,4]）矩约束限制每个权重的更新步长大小，衍生出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制，即Gmake。实验表明，在信任域约束较弱时四阶矩（峰度估计）实现收益最大，而当约束增强时二阶矩实现更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩估计优化器在实践中表现出色，但其行为缺乏统一的数学解释框架。作者希望开发一个信任域框架来统一理解这些机制的运作原理，特别是从矩估计的角度揭示其底层结构。

Method: 构建信任域框架，其中每个权重的更新步长大小受p阶（p∈[2,4]）矩约束限制；由此推导出基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制族Gmake。当p=4时涉及峰度类估计。该框架统一了矩估计归一化、学习率调度、作为动量的谱低通滤波以及算子级谱归一化。

Result: 在GPT2-124M（训练于FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories）上的实验表明，当信任域约束较弱时，四阶矩实现（峰度估计）能带来最大收益；随着信任域控制逐渐增强，二阶矩实现变得更具竞争力，通常达到比对应四阶矩实现略低的验证损失。

Conclusion: Gmake框架为自适应优化器提供了一个统一的信任域解释视角，揭示了不同矩阶数选择在不同约束强度下的性能权衡：弱约束下高阶级矩（四阶）更优，强约束下二阶矩反而更具优势。这一发现为实践中选择自适应优化器配置提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [124] [Learning to Resolve Neutron Resonances with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04027)
*Nataly R. Panczyk,Athanasios Stamatopoulos,Josef Svoboda,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索将全卷积神经网络引入传统R-Matrix代码，用于自动检测中子透射光谱中的共振峰。虽然分类准确率可达93%，但进一步分析表明该指标高估了模型的泛化能力，模型无法可靠推广到未见过的同位素，未来需要更大更多样的训练数据集并融入物理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R-Matrix代码在拟合中子透射数据时依赖先验评估且需要大量人工干预，中子透射数据复杂且噪声大，传统峰值识别方法难以有效分析。研究旨在加速实验后处理流程并减少对先验评估的依赖性偏差。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对单个数据点进行分类，判断其属于共振区或非共振区。模型在七个透射谱上进行训练和测试，包括两个评估谱和五个实验谱。研究基于PHYSOR 2026的先前分析，并加入了额外的训练数据。

Result: 模型实现约93%的分类准确率，但进一步分析表明该指标夸大了模型的泛化能力。即使加入额外训练数据，模型仍无法可靠地推广到之前未见过的同位素。

Conclusion: 当前方法在泛化性上存在固有局限，未来工作需要：(1)评估更大、更多样化的训练数据集是否能产生可泛化的模型；(2)将中子共振的已知物理特征纳入模型以提高性能。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [125] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Lindblad开放系统原理的多时间尺度回波状态网络（Echo-State Network），通过将循环算子拆分为精确离散化的阻尼旋转模式，实现旋转（相混合）与衰减（记忆遗忘）的独立控制，并直接提供全局稳定性保证，无需post-hoc谱半径缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回波状态网络的储备池将信号混合、记忆保持和稳定性耦合在单一随机循环矩阵中，难以分别调控混频和遗忘，也缺乏直接的全局稳定性保证。现有结构化设计（拓扑、范数保持、泄漏、深度等）虽改善了部分性能，但未能在统一框架下提供可逆混合与不可逆遗忘的分模态控制。

Method: 受Lindblad开放系统动力学启发，构建多时间尺度储备池。循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态组装而成，旋转与衰减为独立设计变量分别控制相位混合与记忆丢失；正交模态混合保持正态性，衰减谱直接决定回波状态稳定性裕度，无需谱半径重新缩放。在十种对齐随机种子下，与标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环和下一代储备池以及紧凑训练GRU进行系统对比。

Result: 在有界NARMA-20上取得最优固定储备池性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低平均误差，在最强线性记忆任务上持平，并在广泛基准上总体保持竞争力。消融实验表明旋转增加状态多样性，耗散提供受控遗忘并改善预测条件性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架提供了一种可解释的循环架构，其中混合、记忆和稳定性是显式且可独立调节的设计变量，弥合了开放系统动力学与结构化状态空间建模之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [126] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Open-World Learning (OWL)油井异常检测流水线下游引入一个大语言模型(LLM)智能体层（基于Qwen3.5-397B-A17B MoE模型，通过NVIDIA NIM服务），旨在填补OWL管道"是什么"但无法解释"为什么"和"接下来该做什么"的可解释性空白。该智能体接受结构化传感器指标和上游分类/新颖性断言，返回自然语言论证、置信度排序的批评意见以及新颖性簇的合并命名。在3W数据集989个真实井段上的三组研究中，该智能体在九类分类上达到35.1% top-1 / 63.9% top-3准确率（95% CI [56.9, 70.4]），七类探测中达到71.7% top-2验证准确率[64.8, 77.6]和0.91精度[0.84, 0.95]，新颖性检测达到89.7% [87.0, 91.9]，且5/7隐藏类具备稳定簇命名。该智能体定位为辅助验证、论证、纠偏和命名的新颖性解释层，而非独立分类器，目标是弥合OWL管道在实际运维部署中的可解释性鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWL油井异常检测管道虽然能回答"发生了什么"，但无法回答"模型为什么这么认为"以及"操作员接下来该做什么"，且新发现的新颖簇缺乏可读的名称。这种解释性缺口阻碍了OWL管道在实际运维环境中的部署。

Method: 在既有OWL管道（自编码器检测→多类分类→马氏距离新颖性检测）的下游附加一个LLM智能体层。该层使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B MoE模型（通过NVIDIA NIM服务部署），接收结构化传感器指标和上游分类/新颖性断言，输出自然语言论证、置信度排序的批评意见，并为新颖性簇生成合并的可读命名。智能体的职责包括：(1)验证上游决策、(2)以传感器语言论证决策、(3)标记不合理标签的分歧、(4)为新颖事件命名。

Result: 在3W数据集989个真实井段上的三组研究结果：九类分类任务top-1准确率35.1%、top-3准确率63.9%（95% CI [56.9, 70.4]）；七类探测任务top-2验证准确率71.7% [64.8, 77.6]，精度0.91 [0.84, 0.95]；新颖性检测准确率89.7% [87.0, 91.9]，其中7个隐藏类中5个能获得稳定的聚类命名。

Conclusion: 该LLM智能体不是独立分类器，而是作为上游OWL方法的配套解释层。它通过传感器证据确认上游决策、用可审计的传感器语言论证判断、在标签不合理时标记分歧、并为聚类的未标记事件赋予可读名称，从而弥合OWL管道在运维场景中部署所面临的解释性鸿沟。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [127] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus是一个完全基于电阻式压力阵列数据回答文本查询的开源模型，在STAG基准（27个物体）上达到0.771±0.062的top-1准确率，超过其监督闭集CNN基线（0.76），且无需训练分类头。关键配方是仅187条训练记录、在144k同传感器无标注帧上的掩码自编码器预训练，以及传感器自身的校准仿射变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电阻式压力阵列是最便宜、最广泛部署的触觉传感器，但现有触觉表征学习几乎全部集中在光学触觉传感器（凝胶变形成像）。作者希望证明压力阵列数据本身也足以支持文本查询的语义理解，并开源相应模型以填补这一空白。

Method: 利用掩码自编码器（MAE）在144k无标注的同传感器帧上进行预训练，仅用187条有标注训练记录监督，结合传感器自身校准仿射变换进行归一化，无需训练分类头，直接通过记忆层（memory layer）对文本查询进行检索式回答。

Result: 在STAG基准四个运行中达到top-1 0.771±0.062（top-3 0.935），匹配并有时超过闭集监督CNN的0.76。误差集中在少数接触歧义类别；与文本目标几何无相关性（Spearman rho≤0.05）；对改写及裸名称查询鲁棒；两帧多样性即可恢复八帧准确率的89%。

Conclusion: Tactus验证了压力阵列数据足以支撑文本查询触觉理解，且小数据配方高效。诚实报告失败：跨传感器预训练池化无增益、视觉协同训练反而降低触觉性能、错误归一化管道静默丢弃97%动态范围却仍产生看似合理的结果。权重、代码和记忆层全部开源。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [128] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对航空发动机剩余使用寿命(RUL)预测的联邦学习场景，系统研究了良性异质性（不同运行条件和故障模式）与对抗性异质性（投毒攻击）两大挑战。通过在C-MAPSS数据集上进行受控实验，发现共享表征个性化能弥合约70%的局部-集中式RMSE差距；物理驱动的传感器值后门攻击对标准平均聚合达94.9%成功率且不降低干净准确率；Krum是唯一能抵御协调攻击者的聚合器；个性化与鲁棒聚合结合可将攻击成功率降至2.8%，但存在精度成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许多个飞机运营商在不共享原始数据的情况下联合训练RUL模型，但面临两类关键挑战：(1)良性异质性——不同运营商面临不同的运行条件和故障模式；(2)对抗性异质性——被攻陷的运营商可能提交投毒更新。现有工作对这两类挑战的综合评估不足，且准确率指标本身无法捕获模型安全性，亟需系统的、面向安全的关键评估。

Method: 使用多任务一维卷积神经网络，在C-MAPSS基准上构造结构性非IID数据划分；对良性异质性比较四种缓解方法（共享表征个性化、近端正则化、服务端重加权等）；对对抗异质性评估五种攻击方法（含物理驱动的传感器值后门）应用于四种聚合方法（标准平均、Krum等）；并组合个性化与鲁棒聚合进行联合评估。

Result: (1)共享表征个性化弥合约70%的局部-集中式RMSE差距，远优于近端正则化(21%)和服务端重加权(10%)；(2)传感器值后门对标准平均聚合达94.9%攻击成功率，且不显著改变干净准确率，证明准确率不能认证安全性；(3)Krum将攻击成功率降低一个数量级，是唯一抵御协调攻击者的聚合器；(4)个性化+鲁棒聚合组合可将攻击成功率降至2.8%，但带来少量精度损失；(5)结果在客户端数量和更难六条件数据集上保持一致。

Conclusion: 在FL驱动的航空RUL预测中，良性异质性可通过共享表征个性化有效缓解，但单独使用个性化无法抵御恶意投毒；必须将个性化与鲁棒聚合（如Krum）结合才能同时应对两种异质性。安全性的评估必须显式检查攻击成功率，仅依赖准确率会产生误导。代码与数据分区已公开以供复现。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [129] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归残差量化（RRQ）框架，通过单一checkpoint支持多种比特精度（2/4/6/8位），从2位量化基模型出发逐步添加2位残差修正来构建更高比特表示，无需校准且构建速度快3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量化方法需要为每个目标比特宽度单独保存checkpoint，无法灵活适配多样化的部署约束（精度、内存、吞吐量之间的权衡），缺乏一种从单一模型导出多种精度的方案。

Method: 将权重表示为低比特量化基（2位）加上一系列量化残差修正序列。从PTQ或RTN得到的2位模型出发，逐步添加通过RTN生成的轻量2位残差来构造4、6、8位表示。该方法无需校准数据，也无需联合多比特优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，完整的all-RTN 2/4/6/8位包在1,293秒内构建完成，比MatGPTQ快3.3倍。在六个近期LLM上的实验显示6位和8位具有竞争力精度，4位精度表现依赖具体模型。

Conclusion: RRQ为PTQ提供了一种高效且灵活的单一checkpoint多精度方案，在保证较高位宽精度竞争力的同时大幅降低构建开销，代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [130] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMP是一种循环感知的多尺度补丁混合器，用于长时序预测。它通过自适应循环学习模块为每个输入窗口单独识别主导频率并生成历史/未来循环分量，无需预设周期长度；通过地平线引导的补丁混合器实现位置相关的精修，让早期补丁获得更广上下文而保持近期信息；并对去循环后的残差进行时间对齐的多分辨率建模。在七个长期预测基准上，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环感知预测器通常在数据集级别选择单一周期，难以应对周期性随时间变化或多个周期共存的情况；补丁模型对所有补丁位置统一处理，忽略了距离预测边界远近所导致的上下文需求差异；去循环后的残余动态跨越多个时间分辨率，单尺度难以充分刻画。

Method: CAMP包含三个核心模块：(1) 自适应循环学习模块——对每个输入窗口分别识别主导频率并生成历史与未来的循环分量，无需预定义周期长度；(2) 地平线引导补丁混合器——引入位置相关的精修机制，早期补丁融入更广泛的时序上下文，同时保留接近预测边界的信息；(3) 多分辨率残差建模——通过时间对齐的多尺度表示捕捉去循环后不同尺度的互补动态。

Result: 在七个长期预测基准上，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE；在四个PEMS交通基准的十六个设置中，获得最高的MSE获胜次数。

Conclusion: CAMP通过窗口级别的自适应循环识别、位置感知的补丁精修和多尺度残差建模，有效解决了单周期假设、补丁位置处理不区分以及多分辨率动态描述不足等关键问题，在长期时序预测任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [131] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LaPrune，一种数学上具有精确预算的可微分分层，通过LapSum屏障保持选择质量，并通过归一化二阶矩约束将掩码从密集等质量分配推向硬top-k选择，实现稀疏模型的硬选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k选择决定了稀疏模型中哪些组件保持活跃。硬选择会阻塞梯度，而连续松弛方法往往将掩码的硬度与选择质量耦合在一起，难以独立控制。需要一种既能精确控制预算又能保证梯度可传的方法。

Method: 提出LaPrune可微分层，包含两部分：1）LapSum屏障用于保持选择质量；2）归一化二阶矩约束用于在每个预算下将掩码从密集等质量分配推向硬top-k。作者还推导了饱和比例的种群预测、近二元极限定律以及近零比例的紧最坏情况保证。

Result: 归一化硬度参数对分数尺度具有不变性，而固定的LapSum温度则不具备这种不变性。提供了饱和比例的数学预测、近二元极限定律以及紧的最坏情况保证。

Conclusion: LaPrune实现了精确预算下的可微分硬选择，其归一化硬度参数具有尺度不变性，相比固定温度方法更具鲁棒性，能够在保持梯度可传导的同时逼近硬top-k行为。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>


### [132] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SJEPA是一种无重建的JEPA框架，通过联合学习预测性表示和符号-神经混合转移模型，学习可被简洁符号描述描述的动力学，并在预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性和符号-神经分配之间实现可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构虽然能通过预测目标嵌入来学习抽象状态，但其转移模型通常是不透明的神经网络映射，缺乏可解释性。作者希望学习预测性表示的同时，使诱导的动力学能够被紧凑的符号描述所刻画，以提高模型的透明度和可理解性。

Method: SJEPA采用混合转移模型，将符号律与正则化的神经修正相结合（后者用于处理所选语法之外的动力学）。核心原则是通过两个机制学习最简单且充分的动力学：表示约束保持信息丰富、非坍塌的预测坐标；算子压缩偏好低复杂度的符号-神经转移。作者形式化了诱导动力学复杂度概念，分析了预测坐标的非可辨识性，并证明无约束的算子压缩会直接导致表示坍塌。框架支持交替的表示-方程联合学习和针对固定表示的符号动力学拟合两种模式。

Result: 在受控的钟摆实验中，联合学习发现的符号动力学比事后拟合更简单，且具有更低的长期滚动误差和发散度；无约束的一步诊断实现了预测的坍塌捷径。在语法错误指定的情况下，修正正则化保留了可表示的符号机制，并将神经组件导向残差动力学。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性和符号-神经分配之间存在一个可控的权衡，并验证了SJEPA框架在发现可解释符号动力学方面的有效性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [133] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG
