TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA-KV方法，将KV缓存量化建模为以注意力乘积误差为失真的变换编码问题，推导出闭式最优变换和等体积分组量化方案，在每元素2比特下恢复大部分长上下文检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码每一步都需要加载KV缓存，加载时间超过注意力计算时间，导致推理通过量受限于带宽。减小缓存大小可以同时提升解码速度和服务器容量，关键在于在每元素固定比特数约束下减少缓存大小并保持注意力乘积精度。现有基于正交变换的方法要么是数据无关的，要么没有从失真准则推导变换，且依赖随机或Hadamard旋转与固定宽度标量量化，在低码率下次优。

Method: 将KV缓存量化形式化为变换编码问题，失真定义为注意力乘积误差。从校准统计中推导键和值的闭式最优变换（基于高分辨率模型），证明最优键变换非正交且满足广义Parseval关系，使注意力感知失真退化为变换域中的MSE。从而可以直接使用MSE最优的向量量化器应用于变换后的键系数。为满足固定宽度布局，将系数分组为等体积分区，使等大小码本在高分辨率模型下达到变速率最优。

Result: 在每元素2比特下，NOVA-KV相比标量量化方法恢复了大部分长上下文检索精度损失，同时保持相当的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过注意力感知的变换编码框架和等体积分组的向量量化策略，NOVA-KV在极低比特率下实现了KV缓存压缩与解码性能的良好平衡，超越了现有的正交变换+标量量化方案。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [134] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for Traffic Intelligence in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04075)
*Laha Ale,Letian Lin,Na Cao,Zheng Ma,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空图Transformer框架，用于边缘计算场景下的流量预测。该框架通过图神经网络捕捉服务区域间的空间相关性，并利用Transformer自注意力机制学习长期时间依赖模式，将空间表示学习与时间推理解耦。在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的实验表明，该框架在多个预测时域上优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等基于循环图网络的基线方法，能有效支持主动式资源预配置并降低过载风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中的流量预测对主动式资源管理至关重要，但现有基于循环神经网络的预测方法虽能捕捉短期动态，却难以在非平稳条件下建模长期时间演化。此外，实际蜂窝边缘系统中的流量数据同时呈现空间相关性和长期时间依赖性，需要联合建模两者的方法。

Method: 提出一个时空图Transformer框架：(1) 使用图神经网络（GNN）捕捉服务区域之间的空间相关性；(2) 利用Transformer的自注意力机制从历史流量观测中学习长期时间模式；(3) 将空间表示学习与时间推理解耦，以支持大规模时空流量建模。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的图Transformer在多个预测时域上持续优于基于循环图网络的基线方法（GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM、GCN-GRU），生成的预测结果相比反应式管理策略能实现更有效的主动资源预配置并降低过载风险。

Conclusion: 图增强的注意力机制（即图与Transformer的结合）在构建智能自适应的边缘计算系统方面具有显著潜力，能够更好地应对非平稳条件下的大规模时空流量预测挑战。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting is essential for proactive resource management in edge computing, where service demand evolves dynamically across both space and time. In practical cellular edge systems, traffic exhibits strong spatial correlations among neighboring service regions and long-range temporal dependencies driven by user mobility and application behavior. Existing recurrent forecasting approaches can capture short-term dynamics but often struggle to model long-horizon traffic evolution under non-stationary conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a spatiotemporal graph Transformer framework that jointly models spatial interactions and temporal dependencies for traffic forecasting in edge computing. The framework employs graph neural networks to capture spatial correlations among service regions and leverages Transformer-based self-attention to learn long-range temporal patterns from historical traffic observations. By decoupling spatial representation learning from temporal reasoning, the proposed approach provides an effective mechanism for large-scale spatiotemporal traffic modeling. Extensive experiments on a real-world cellular network dataset demonstrate that the proposed graph Transformer consistently outperforms recurrent graph-based baselines, including GCN-RNN, GCN-LSTM, and GCN-GRU models, across multiple forecasting horizons. The resulting forecasts enable more effective proactive resource provisioning and reduce overload risk compared with reactive management strategies. These results highlight the potential of graph-enhanced attention mechanisms for building intelligent and adaptive edge computing systems.

</details>


### [135] [SpecDrop: Parameter-Free Category-Conditioned Routing for Modular Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04084)
*Boyao Wang,Zhihan Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecDrop，一种无参数、固定权重的路由方案，用于检验混合专家（MoE）网络中路由帮助的条件。研究发现当训练信号粒度与目标类别对齐时（如图像有单一超类标签），SpecDrop超越参数匹配的基线；而在模糊划分（多个类别跨越训练单元）时，路由机制退化到与No-Routing基线无差异。结论是粒度对齐而非算法选择决定了路由是否有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE网络通过学习的路由器、门控和负载均衡损失追求专业化，但在总参数预算匹配的情况下，学习到的路由器可能表现不及等权重的无路由基线。作者质疑瓶颈在于路由算法本身，还是训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐程度。

Method: SpecDrop是一种固定且无参数的路由方案：每个分支对指定类别获得权重p_a，对其余类别获得小泄漏p_i>0，通过类别无关的固定分母合并，无学习路由参数、无辅助损失，推理时需类别标签。在CIFAR-100（ResNet-110）和ImageNet-1K（ViT-S/16）等单超类标签视觉任务，以及SlimPajama-6B语言建模和SuperNI指令微调等模糊划分任务上进行实验。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上达79.23%，ImageNet-1K上达79.89%，超过参数匹配且不使用标签的基线（CIFAR-100比稠密高出+4.75，ImageNet-1K比No-Routing+SE控制高出+6.53）。但给定相同标签时，掩蔽稠密模型输出的方式在纯准确率上更强（85.2/83.7）。SpecDrop的贡献在于将标签转化为训练好的模块化结构：58%/100%的分支-类别对齐，掩蔽增益为0.00（CIFAR）/ +1.06（ImageNet）。在模糊划分上，路由机制在种子噪声范围内退化为匹配的No-Routing对照组。

Conclusion: 粒度对齐而非算法选择决定了路由何时有效。SpecDrop将标签转化为训练中的模块化结构，验证了当训练信号粒度与目标类别对齐时路由才有帮助的理论。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks pursue specialization through learned routers, gates, and load-balancing losses, yet at matched total-parameter budgets learned routers can underperform equal-weight No-Routing baselines. Is the bottleneck the routing algorithm, or the alignment between training-signal granularity and the target categories? We probe the question with SpecDrop, a fixed parameter-free routing scheme: each of $K$ branches receives weight $p_a$ for its assigned category and a small leakage $p_i > 0$ otherwise, merged through a category-independent fixed denominator, with no learned routing parameters and no auxiliary losses; the category label is required at inference. On vision tasks where each image has one superclass label (CIFAR-100 on ResNet-110; ImageNet-1K on ViT-S/16), SpecDrop reaches 79.23% on CIFAR-100 and 79.89% on ImageNet-1K, exceeding parameter-matched baselines that do not use the label (+4.75 over dense on CIFAR-100; +6.53 over the No-Routing+SE control on ImageNet-1K). These gains quantify what category supervision buys when deployed through routing -- not an advantage over label-aware deployments of the baselines: given the same label, masking a dense model's outputs is stronger for accuracy alone (85.2 / 83.7). SpecDrop's contribution is converting the label into trained-in modular structure: 58%/100% branch-category alignment, and masking gains of 0.00 (CIFAR) / +1.06 (ImageNet) -- the output-space restriction is largely internalized during training. On fuzzy partitions, where training units span multiple categories (SlimPajama-6B language modeling with a 30M Transformer; SuperNI instruction tuning over Llama-3.2-1B with LoRA), the routing mechanism reduces to the matched No-Routing controls within seed noise, the null our thesis predicts. Granularity alignment, not algorithm choice, localizes when routing helps. Code: https://github.com/Beryex/SpecDrop

</details>


### [136] [Out-Of-The-Loop Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04113)
*Gustavo Sutter,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有多保真贝叶斯优化（MF-BO）算法在处理"真实最高保真度函数过于昂贵而无法纳入优化循环，但已有历史金标准数据"的场景时存在次优性问题，并提出通过结合任务描述符来利用历史高保真度数据加以改进，在合成函数、化学和超参数优化等真实任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多保真贝叶斯优化中，许多重要任务的最高保真度函数成本极高，无法直接纳入优化循环。然而实践中常有来自历史实验的金标准（最高保真度）数据可用，例如化学家使用计算机模拟选取top-k候选分子后再揭示其真实目标值。标准MF-BO算法无法有效利用这些历史数据，导致在真实场景下表现次优。

Method: 首先在理想假设下证明标准MF-BO算法在所述真实场景中的次优性；然后提出一种方法，将历史高保真度数据与任务描述符（可显式给出或从非结构化元数据中提取）相结合，融入到多保真贝叶斯优化框架中，以利用跨任务的相关性来改进当前任务的优化性能。

Result: 在合成函数以及化学和超参数优化等真实世界问题上验证了所提方法的有效性，表明结合历史高保真度数据与任务描述符能够显著优于现有的标准MF-BO方法。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了标准MF-BO在存在历史高保真数据场景下的局限性，并提出利用任务描述符将历史数据纳入优化框架的有效解决方案，为科学和工程中的昂贵黑箱优化问题提供了一条实用且可推广的改进路径。

Abstract: Black-box optimization is a ubiquitous problem in science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions with cheaper lower-fidelity proxies available. Multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MF-BO) is a principled approach to this problem, leveraging correlations across different fidelities when querying the objective. However, for many important MF-BO tasks, the true highest-fidelity function is prohibitively expensive to be part of the optimization loop. Nevertheless, practitioners often have gold standard data (observations of the highest-fidelity function) obtained from previous experiments that might provide information for the current task. For instance, in molecular optimization, chemists often pick the top-$k$ candidate molecules using various computer simulations, and later reveal their true objective function values. In this work, we demonstrate the suboptimality of standard MF-BO algorithms in the real-world scenarios above, even under ideal assumptions. Next, we mitigate this problem by incorporating historical high-fidelity data accompanied by task descriptors---which can be explicitly given or extracted from unstructured metadata. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods on synthetic functions, as well as real-world problems in chemistry and hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [137] [LiNC: Lightweight Noise Correction via Per-Sample Trust and Gaussian Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04147)
*Abhishek Moturu,Babak Taati,Anna Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiNC是一种轻量级噪声校正方法，为每个训练样本添加一个可训练的置信参数，通过观察标签与模型预测的凸组合来学习何时信任真实标签、何时依赖模型，并利用高斯混合模型将样本分为干净、模糊和噪声三类进行软/硬校正，在MedMNISTv2的10个2D数据集上（最高50%标签噪声）稳定提升准确率并有效检测误标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据集普遍存在标签噪声（如标注者间差异、标注错误、模糊病例），这会严重损害据此训练的机器学习模型的可靠性与临床有效性。

Method: 每个训练样本引入一个可训练的信任参数；训练损失为观察标签与模型预测分布的凸组合，由该信任参数控制。梯度分析显示干净样本与噪声样本的信任值在训练早期朝相反方向演变，形成可分离的分布。用三成分高斯混合模型将信任值分为干净、模糊、噪声三类，对噪声类执行短时软校正阶段，再执行最终硬校正阶段。

Result: 在MedMNISTv2的10个2D数据集上、噪声比例最高50%的条件下，持续获得准确率提升，并具备强大的误标检测能力；训练时间复杂度仍由基础网络主导，额外内存随训练集规模线性增长，渐近开销可忽略。

Conclusion: LiNC通过极低的额外开销（每个样本仅一个标量参数）即可有效校正医学影像中的标签噪声，兼顾准确率提升与误标检测，具有良好的实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Label noise is common in medical imaging datasets due to factors such as inter-rater variability, annotation errors, and ambiguous cases. This can severely undermine the reliability and clinical effectiveness of machine learning models trained using those datasets. To address this challenge, we introduce Lightweight Noise Correction (LiNC), which adds a single trainable trust parameter per training sample and learns when to use the observed label and when to defer to the model during a standard training loop. The key idea is to train using a convex combination of the observed label and the model's own predictive distribution, controlled by a per-sample trust parameter. We show that the gradient of this objective drives trust values in opposite directions for clean versus noisy samples in the early training phase, yielding separable trust distributions. We use a 3-component Gaussian Mixture Model over the trust values to separate them into clean, ambiguous, and noisy cases and then execute a short soft-correction phase on the noisy cases and a final hard correction phase. Experiments on ten 2D datasets from MedMNISTv2 under label noise of up to 50% show consistent gains in accuracy and strong mislabel detection. LiNC adds negligible asymptotic overhead: the training-time complexity remains dominated by the base network, with additional memory growing linearly with the size of the training set.

</details>


### [138] [MINT: Tensor Decomposition on Stacked Recurrence Matrices for Time Series Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04157)
*Kaamil Kaka,Audrey Der,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Zachary Zimmerman,Vikram Jayaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出张量化自相似矩阵（tensorized self-similarity matrices）作为一种新的时间序列数据挖掘原语，通过计算大小为N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1)的矩阵点积张量，并利用张量分解方法挖掘共聚类模式，在轨道交通、电力需求、风力涡轮机和汽车交通等数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归图（Recurrence Plots）虽已广泛应用于多种领域（如星光线曲线、声音波形、CCT遥测），但现有方法在处理大规模单变量时间序列数据集以及跨传感器共聚类模式挖掘方面存在局限。作者希望提出一种更具扩展性的原语，能够自然地处理多变量数据并有效挖掘跨传感器的重复模式。

Method: 该方法将时间序列数据集表示为N个长度为n的单变量序列，使用长度为m的子序列窗口计算点积自相似矩阵，生成大小为N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1)的三阶张量。随后利用张量分解方法对生成的张量进行挖掘，以发现共聚类的跨传感器模式。该方法命名为MINT管线，其张量化的性质天然支持扩展到多变量数据集。

Result: 在公共交通（mass rapid transit）、电力需求、风力涡轮机和汽车交通四类真实数据集上进行了验证，结果表明MINT管线能够有效地对高度规则的数据集中包含规律间隔motif的跨传感器模式进行共聚类。

Conclusion: 张量化自相似矩阵作为一种新的时间序列数据挖掘原语是有效的，MINT管线能够在规则性强的真实数据中成功发现跨传感器的共聚类模式，为大规模时间序列分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Recurrence plots are a time series data mining primitive applied to a variety of domains (e.g. star light curves, sound waveforms, CCT telemetry). This work proposes tensorized self-similarity matrices as a primitive for univariate time series datasets ($N\times n$) of $N$ time series of length $n$ with a subsequence window of length $m$, and whose tensor-based nature is naturally extensible to multivariate datasets. The proposed method to compute this primitive computes dot plots of size $N \times (n-m+1) \times (n-m+ 1)$ from these datasets, where the subsequent tensor is mined using tensor decomposition methods to mine for co-clustered patterns. We demonstrate our results in mass rapid transit, electricity demand, wind turbine, and car traffic data, finding the MINT pipeline effectively co-clusters cross-sensor patterns in highly regular datasets containing motifs at regular intervals.

</details>


### [139] [Understanding Fault Tolerance of Adversarially Robust Pruned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04173)
*Manali Dangarikar,Cory Merkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了剪枝、对抗训练和硬件故障注入三者对卷积神经网络鲁棒性的交互影响。使用MNIST上的三层CNN，发现对抗训练在提升输入扰动鲁棒性的同时增加了对权重故障的敏感性，而剪枝对故障敏感性影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络部署在资源受限的神经形态硬件上时，同时面临三个挑战：剪枝压缩、对抗扰动脆弱性以及硬件权重故障（如卡零错误）。虽然这些因素各自已被研究，但它们的组合效应对模型可靠性的影响鲜有关注。本文旨在填补这一空白，实证考察三者交互作用。

Method: 使用紧凑的三层CNN模型在MNIST数据集上进行三项实验：(1) 对比自然训练与对抗训练模型在同时遭受硬件故障和对抗攻击时的容错能力；(2) 评估剪枝对对抗鲁棒性的影响；(3) 表征跨故障率、对抗扰动幅度和剪枝水平的联合精度曲面。

Result: 对抗训练虽然提升了对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但增加了对卡零权重故障的敏感性。与直觉相反，剪枝并未显著增加故障敏感性，且不同剪枝水平在故障率和攻击强度变化下对模型影响很小。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调需要联合考虑对抗鲁棒性和硬件可靠性，不能孤立地优化单一鲁棒性指标。剪枝在故障和攻击联合场景下表现出出乎意料的稳定性，而对抗训练在硬件故障下的脆弱性需要进一步关注。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) deployed on resource-constrained neuromorphic hardware face three concurrent challenges: the need for model compression through pruning, vulnerability to adversarial input perturbations, and susceptibility to hardware-induced weight faults such as stuck-at-zero errors. While each of these factors has been studied in isolation, their combined effects on model reliability have received little attention. This paper presents an empirical investigation of how pruning, adversarial training, and hardware fault injection interact to affect the robustness of convolutional neural networks. Using a compact three-layer CNN trained on MNIST, we conduct three experiments: (1) comparing the fault tolerance of naturally and adversarially trained models under simultaneous hardware faults and adversarial attacks, (2) evaluating how pruning affects adversarial robustness, and (3) characterizing the joint accuracy surface across fault rates, adversarial perturbation magnitudes, and pruning levels. Our results show that adversarial training improves robustness against input perturbations but increases sensitivity to stuck-at-zero weight faults. Contrary to intuition, pruning did not significantly increase fault sensitivity, and varying the pruning level had little effect across fault rates and attack strengths. These results highlight the need to jointly consider adversarial robustness and hardware reliability.

</details>


### [140] [TS2TabPFN: Time Series Classification and Extrinsic Regression through Feature Extraction and a Tabular Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04174)
*Gabriel da Costa Merlin,Diego Furtado Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS2TabPFN框架，将显式特征提取与TabPFN 2.5表格数据基础模型相结合，用于时间序列分类（TSC）和外部回归（TSER）任务，在TSER上显著超越现有最先进模型，并在TSC上超过大多数最佳算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列方法可分为基于特征的方法和基于深度学习的端到端方法，前者强调特征提取质量与人工控制，后者强调复杂架构对原始数据的自动预测能力，二者之间存在鸿沟，缺乏将特征工程的可控性与端到端模型的自动化性能结合的统一框架。

Method: 将显式特征提取模块与TabPFN 2.5（面向表格数据的先进基础模型）集成，利用特征提取提供的结构化表示，再交由TabPFN进行预测，从而桥接传统特征工程与端到端模型之间的差距。

Result: 大量实验表明，TS2TabPFN在TSER任务上以统计显著性超越现有最先进模型；在TSC任务上亦超过大多数当前最佳性能算法，且具备鲁棒性和高效性。

Conclusion: 将基础模型与结构化特征相结合能够克服单一范式的局限性，为时间序列分析确立了新的最先进水平，验证了跨范式融合方法的有效性。

Abstract: Time series data are ubiquitous in practical applications, where classification (TSC) and extrinsic regression (TSER) have emerged as essential tasks for obtaining value from temporal sequences. While the literature has seen significant progress through feature-based and deep learning models, existing methods often focus either on the quality of feature extraction or on the intrinsic predictive power of complex architectures applied to raw data. This division creates a gap between the control offered by feature engineering and the automated performance of end-to-end models. This paper proposes TS2TabPFN, a framework that bridges this gap by integrating explicit feature extraction with TabPFN 2.5, a cutting-edge foundation model for tabular data, to leverage its predictive capabilities. Our extensive experimental evaluation demonstrates that TS2TabPFN significantly outperforms state-of-the-art models in TSER tasks with statistical significance, providing a robust and efficient alternative for TSC and surpassing most of the currently best-performing algorithms. These results suggest that combining foundation models with structured features overcomes single-paradigm limitations, establishing a new time series state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [141] [A Comparative Study of Feature Selection Methods for EHR Diagnosis Codes in Opioid Use Disorder Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04180)
*Zihan Ding,Yinan Liu,Tengfei Ma,Rachel Wong,George Leibowitz,Benjamin Littenberg,Xia Zheng,Richard N. Rosenthal,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文聚焦电子健康记录(EHR)中基于诊断相关特征的特征选择问题，比较了五种特征选择范式（复发富集、NTK启发的早期梯度敏感性、LightGBM-SHAP、弹性网络和LLM引导的语义选择）用于阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）预测，并通过统一的预处理与评估框架考察下游预测性能、重采样稳定性及罕见诊断码的表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR数据通常是高维、稀疏、含噪且冗余的，大型特征集会增加计算负担和过拟合风险，并降低模型可解释性，从而限制其在临床场景中的实用性。因此迫切需要系统比较不同特征选择范式，以为临床预测建模提供可靠的方法论指导。

Method: 基于统一的预处理和评估框架，对五种特征选择范式进行对比：1）复发富集法；2）基于NTK（神经正切核）的早期梯度敏感性方法；3）LightGBM-SHAP；4）弹性网络（Elastic Net）；5）大语言模型(LLM)引导的语义特征选择。所有方法聚焦于诊断相关特征，并以统一标准评估其下游预测性能、重采样稳定性以及对低频诊断码的表征能力。

Result: 研究发现预测性能随特征预算增大而提升，但超过中等规模后收益递减。NTK敏感性方法在准确性和稳定性之间取得最佳平衡；LLM引导的选择虽然独立性能较低，但能补充具有临床意义的互补信号。

Conclusion: 不同特征选择范式各有优劣：NTK敏感性是最佳的综合方案，LLM引导方法可作为补充手段提供临床语义价值。研究为EHR场景下特征选择的实践提供了系统的实证比较和选择参考。

Abstract: Feature selection is a critical step in electronic health record (EHR)-based predictive modeling, where input variables are often high-dimensional, sparse, noisy, and redundant. Large feature sets not only increase computational burden and overfitting risk, but also make model interpretation difficult, leading to limited usefulness in clinical settings. In this study, we focus on diagnosis-related features and compare five feature selection paradigms for opioid use disorder (OUD) prediction: recurrence enrichment, NTK-motivated early gradient sensitivity, LightGBM-SHAP, Elastic Net, and large language model (LLM)-guided semantic selection. We use a unified preprocessing and evaluation framework and assess each method by downstream predictive performance, resampling stability, and representation of infrequent diagnosis codes. Our results demonstrate that performance improves with larger feature budgets with diminishing returns beyond a moderate size. NTK sensitivity provides the best overall balance of accuracy and stability, and LLM-guided selection contributes complementary clinically meaningful signals despite lower standalone performance.

</details>


### [142] [Unscented KalmanNet: a hybrid deep learning filter with calibrated posterior covariance for nonlinear state estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04201)
*Minhyeok Ko,Abdollah Shafieezadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unscented KalmanNet (UKN)，一种混合递归估计器，在UKF的显式sigma-point协方差递归基础上，引入两个结构上不同的学习组件（NoiseNet和GainNet），分别预测有界乘性噪声协方差校正和增益残差校正，并通过校准感知的训练目标联合优化精度与校准。在三个合成系统和UZH-FPV真实飞行数据上，UKN相比UKF在合成案例中RMSE降低26.4%-49.7%，在真实数据上位置和速度RMSE分别降低22.4%和34.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动态系统的状态估计通常使用UKF，但在实践中，未知且时变的噪声统计和模型失配会降低估计精度和协方差校准。现有的学习型滤波器大多基于EKF构建，要么放弃显式协方差，要么学习不确定性但不修正失配导致增益偏差。因此需要一种既能保持UKF显式sigma-point协方差性质，又能通过学习修正噪声统计和增益偏差的混合方法。

Method: UKN通过两个结构上不同的学习组件增强UKF：(1) NoiseNet——将有界乘性校正应用于固定基线来预测时变的过程和测量协方差，保证正定性；(2) GainNet——对解析增益施加有界残差校正。训练目标采用校准感知设计，结合状态误差、协方差一致性和创新一致性项，并通过自适应权重联合优化精度与校准。

Result: UKN在三个合成系统和UZH-FPV真实飞行数据上与UKF、KalmanNet、Bayesian KalmanNet对比。在所有四个示例中取得了最低的聚合状态估计误差；合成案例相对UKF的RMSE降低26.4%-49.7%；对11次飞行进行留一序列交叉验证显示平均位置和速度RMSE分别降低22.4%和34.3%；UKN还展现出最低的折间变异性，归一化NEES和经验覆盖率最接近标称值。

Conclusion: UKN通过在保持UKF显式协方差递归的同时引入NoiseNet和GainNet两个学习组件，实现了精度和协方差校准的同时提升。实验证明其在合成和真实数据上均优于现有滤波器，具有更好的估计精度、校准质量和稳定性。

Abstract: State estimation for nonlinear dynamical systems is commonly performed with the Unscented Kalman filter (UKF), which propagates the state moments through deterministic sigma points and reports a posterior covariance at every step. In practice, however, unknown and time-varying noise statistics and model mismatch degrade both estimation accuracy and covariance calibration. Existing learned filters improve accuracy but are largely built on the extended Kalman filter and either forgo an explicit covariance or learn uncertainty without correcting mismatch-induced gain bias. This paper introduces the Unscented KalmanNet (UKN), a hybrid recursive estimator that augments the UKF with two structurally distinct learned components while preserving its explicit sigma-point covariance recursion. NoiseNet predicts time-varying process and measurement covariances as bounded multiplicative corrections to fixed baselines, guaranteeing positive definiteness, while GainNet applies a bounded residual correction to the analytical gain. A calibration-aware training objective combines state error with covariance- and innovation-consistency terms through adaptive weights, jointly optimizing accuracy and calibration. UKN is benchmarked against UKF, KalmanNet, and Bayesian KalmanNet on three synthetic systems and UZH-FPV real-flight data. It achieves the lowest aggregate state-estimation error in all four examples and reduces RMSE by $26.4$-$49.7\%$ compared to UKF in the synthetic cases. Leave-one-sequence-out cross-validation over 11 flights shows $22.4\%$ and $34.3\%$ reductions in mean position and velocity RMSE, respectively. UKN also yields the lowest fold-to-fold variability, with normalized NEES and empirical coverage closest to nominal values among the other filters.

</details>


### [143] [From Non-Convex Self-Concordant Regularization to Scalable Quasi-Newton Training of PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04206)
*Chenhao Si,Kang An,Shiqian Ma,Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCORE，一种受自和谐启发的拟牛顿方法，用于物理信息神经网络（PINN）训练。其核心机制是利用从学到的逆度量中计算出的单一拟牛顿递减量，同时决定强Wolfe测试的候选步长和用于定义下一割线几何的自适应偏移量，从而在无需Hessian构造或Hessian-vector乘积的情况下实现高精度求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN训练通常需要高精度的拟牛顿细化才能获得可靠的偏微分方程解，但其残差目标可能存在不定性、近似奇异性以及不良缩放的局部曲率，这给标准优化方法带来了挑战。

Method: SCORE融合了正则化拟牛顿方法的割线模型稳定机制和自和谐方法的局部度量步长选择规则。它利用单一拟牛顿递减量同时控制候选步长和自适应偏移量，偏移位移代表沿接受步长的平均偏移度量作用，且无需Hessian构造或Hessian-vector乘积。通过强Wolfe接受、回溯线搜索和标准曲率保护实现全局化。

Result: 在粘性Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、Korteweg-de Vries和复Ginzburg-Landau方程上的实验表明，SCORE比BFGS和自缩放Broyden基线获得更低的最终误差。Burgers消融实验进一步显示，偏移曲率稳定化和递减量步长选择对高精度细化具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: SCORE在局部谱等价条件下，其拟牛顿递减量和候选步长与正偏移度量中的对应量保持可比性；在匹配度量情形下恢复归一化自和谐规则。实验证据表明该方法在不修改PINN目标的前提下有效提升了训练精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often require high-accuracy quasi-Newton refinement to obtain reliable partial differential equation solutions, but their residual objectives can exhibit indefinite, nearly singular, and poorly scaled local curvature. Regularized quasi-Newton methods provide established mechanisms for stabilizing secant models, while self-concordant methods provide local-metric rules for curvature-dependent step selection. Building on these two lines of work, we propose SCORE, a self-concordance-inspired quasi-Newton method with decrement-coupled shifted secant geometry for PINN training. Its distinguishing mechanism is that a single quasi-Newton decrement computed from the learned inverse metric jointly determines a strong-Wolfe-tested candidate step and an adaptive shift used to define the next secant geometry. The shifted displacement represents the action of an averaged shifted metric along the accepted step, while requiring neither Hessian construction nor Hessian-vector products. Under a local spectral-equivalence condition, we show that the quasi-Newton decrement and candidate step remain comparable to their counterparts in a positive shifted metric, and recover the normalized self-concordant rule in the matched-metric case. Strong Wolfe acceptance, fallback line search, and standard curvature safeguards provide globalization without modifying the underlying PINN objective. Experiments on the viscous Burgers, Kuramoto--Sivashinsky, Korteweg--de Vries, and complex Ginzburg--Landau equations show that SCORE attains lower final errors than the tested BFGS and self-scaled Broyden baselines. The Burgers ablation further indicates that shifted curvature stabilization and decrement-based step selection make complementary contributions to high-accuracy refinement.

</details>


### [144] [Attention-Only White-Box Transformer via LeJEPA-Based Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04213)
*Yang Bai,Linyuan Wang,Haoyang Jiang,Nuolin Sun,Libin Hou,Bin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从联合视角重新审视白盒网络推导与自监督学习，将LeJEPA自监督框架中的各向同性高斯嵌入分布假设与稀疏率缩减目标的扩张项R(Z)概念对齐，利用LeJEPA优化R(Z)，并通过ADMM推导出仅含注意力机制的白盒Transformer（无需ISTA结构或MLP层）。该模型在CIFAR-10上达到88.88%、CIFAR-100上达63.54%的分类准确率，与原始CRATE（89.18%/63.56%）性能接近但参数量减少约31%。此外，在标准ViT中发现用ReLU激活替换所有MLP块可去除约66%参数且保持竞争性能，暗示标准ViT中MLP模块可能存在冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将白盒网络的优化算法推导与自监督学习范式相互解耦，本文旨在从联合视角重新审视二者，探索自监督学习如何指导白盒Transformer的结构设计，从而在保持可解释性的同时提升参数效率。

Method: 将LeJEPA自监督框架假设的最优嵌入分布（各向同性高斯分布）与稀疏率缩减目标中的扩张项R(Z)建立概念等价关系；利用LeJEPA优化R(Z)，并通过ADMM算法推导剩余项R^c(Z|U_[K])+λ‖Z‖_0，从而构造仅含注意力模块的白盒Transformer，去除原设计中的ISTA结构或MLP层。此外，在标准ViT中探索用ReLU激活替换MLP块的影响。

Result: 在Base规模下，所提出的仅注意力白盒Transformer在CIFAR-10上获得88.88%准确率、CIFAR-100上获得63.54%，与原始CRATE（89.18%/63.56%）性能相当，但参数量减少约31%。对标准ViT的额外研究表明，在知识蒸馏下用ReLU替换所有MLP块可移除约66%参数且保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: 自监督学习范式与白盒网络优化可以从联合视角统一设计，实现性能几乎无损而参数量显著减少的注意力-only白盒Transformer；同时对标准ViT中MLP模块冗余性的探索揭示了进一步简化Transformer架构的潜力。

Abstract: Existing studies on self-supervised learning for white-box networks typically decouple the derivation of white-box networks via optimization algorithms from self-supervised learning paradigms. In this work, we instead revisit the two components from a joint perspective. The LeJEPA-based self-supervised framework assumes an isotropic Gaussian distribution as the optimal embedding distribution for downstream tasks, which is conceptually equivalent to the expansion term $R(Z)$ in the sparse rate reduction objective guiding white-box Transformer optimization. Building on this observation, we use the LeJEPA self-supervised paradigm to optimize $R(Z)$, and derive the remaining terms $R^{c}(Z\mid U_{[K]})+λ\lVert Z\rVert_{0}$ via the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) into an attention-only Transformer that dispenses with the ISTA structure or MLP layers of the original design. Experimental results demonstrate that our attention-only white-box Transformer achieves classification accuracies of $88.88\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $63.54\%$ on CIFAR-100 at the Base scale under the LeJEPA self-supervised paradigm, while the original white-box Transformer CRATE achieves classification accuracies of $89.18\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $63.56\%$ on CIFAR-100. Our model achieves competitive performance while reducing the parameter count by roughly $31\%$. Beyond the white-box setting, we further investigate standard ViTs and find that replacing all MLP blocks with ReLU activations under knowledge distillation removes approximately 66\% of the parameters while preserving competitive accuracy, motivating further investigation into the potential redundancy of MLP modules in standard ViT architectures.

</details>


### [145] [Random features for Grassmannian kernel approximation with bounded rank-one projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04227)
*Rémi Delogne,Laurent Jacques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族基于秩一投影的随机特征映射方法，用于在Grassmannian流形（低维子空间）上实现可扩展的核机器。该方法通过秩一投影子空间投影矩阵并施加有界非线性变换（周期或二值），使得特征空间中的内积近似旋转不变的Grassmannian核，且仅依赖于子空间之间的主角度，从而避免了经典投影核和Binet-Cauchy核所需的完整Gram矩阵计算，大幅降低计算与存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Grassmannian核（如投影核、Binet-Cauchy核）需要计算完整Gram矩阵，对于大规模高维子空间数据集而言计算和内存成本过高，阻碍了其在可扩展核机器中的应用。需要一种能够近似这些核且计算高效的随机特征表示方法。

Method: 提出一族随机特征映射：(1) 对子空间投影矩阵施加秩一投影；(2) 施加有界非线性变换（周期变换或二值变换）以控制输出分布。对于周期变换，近似核具有闭式表达，可在逆Binet-Cauchy与高斯型核之间调节；对于二值变换，生成紧凑的单比特子空间特征。此外，基于随机化快速傅里叶变换的结构化秩一投影可进一步减少计算量。

Result: 理论分析表明，当特征数量相对于子空间固有维度足够大时，特征空间内积能高概率地在所有固定维子空间上一致地近似旋转不变的Grassmannian核。实验在合成数据和ETH-80分类任务上验证了该方法能准确保持Grassmannian几何，同时显著降低计算、内存和存储开销。

Conclusion: 秩一嵌入为经典Grassmannian核提供了一种实用且可扩展的替代方案，在保持几何保真度的同时克服了大规模子空间数据集上的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: We propose a family of random feature maps for scalable kernel machines on low-dimensional subspaces, ie on the Grassmannian manifold. Such representations are useful when data classes or clusters are well described by the span of a few samples. Classical Grassmannian kernels, including the projection and Binet-Cauchy kernels, require full Gram matrices, which leads to prohibitive computational and memory costs for large high-dimensional subspace datasets. We address this limitation using random features based on rank-one projections of subspace projection matrices followed by bounded non-linear transforms, either periodic or binary, to control the resulting distributions.
  We show that inner products in the random feature space approximate well-defined rotation-invariant Grassmannian kernels that depend only on the principal angles between subspaces. When the number of features is sufficiently large relative to the intrinsic subspace dimension, the approximation holds uniformly over all fixed-dimensional subspaces with high probability. For periodic transforms, the approximated kernel has a closed-form expression with tunable behaviour between inverse Binet-Cauchy and Gaussian-type regimes. Binary transforms yield compact one-bit subspace features, although no closed-form kernel is known. Structured rank-one projections based on randomised fast Fourier transforms further reduce computation without sacrificing practical accuracy. Experiments on synthetic data and ETH-80 classification tasks show that these features accurately preserve Grassmannian geometry while reducing computation, memory, and storage. Rank-one embeddings therefore provide a practical and scalable alternative to classical Grassmannian kernels.

</details>


### [146] [Transferable Dual-Stream Representations for Mesoscale-Preserving Sea Surface Temperature Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04230)
*Parth Doshi,Priyanka Aravindan,Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EddyFlow，一个用于千米级海表温度降尺度的表示学习框架，通过物理信息约束在保持预测精度的同时保留多尺度物理结构，在未见过区域的零样本和少样本设置中表现出优异的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习降尺度模型只优化重建误差，导致输出过于平滑，丢失对区域海洋动力学至关重要的中尺度变化。同时，逐像素目标函数和单上下文条件限制了模型保持光谱保真度和跨区域泛化的能力。

Method: EddyFlow是一个物理信息驱动的表示学习框架，在圣劳伦斯湾训练，并在芬迪湾和墨西哥湾进行零样本和少样本评估。该框架在预测精度、尺度依赖结构和区域泛化之间取得平衡，同时保持光谱保真度。

Result: 物理信息表示学习使零样本RMSE降低21%，在未见领域上相对持续性预测的skill达到85.6%，PSD比值约为1.00，保持近理想的光谱保真度。

Conclusion: EddyFlow验证了物理信息表示学习能够在不牺牲预测准确性的前提下，有效保持降尺度输出的多尺度物理结构，并实现跨区域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Deep learning models for scientific spatio-temporal downscaling often minimize reconstruction error while failing to preserve physically meaningful multi-scale structure. For sea surface temperature prediction, this can yield outputs that are numerically plausible yet overly smooth, missing mesoscale variability critical to regional ocean dynamics. Existing methods often focus on pixel-wise objectives or single-context conditioning, which limits their ability to preserve spectral fidelity and generalize across regions. To address this, we propose EddyFlow, a representation learning framework for kilometer-scale sea surface temperature downscaling that balances predictive accuracy, scale-dependent structure, and regional generalization. EddyFlow is trained on the Gulf of St.~Lawrence and evaluated in zero-shot and few-shot settings on the Bay of Fundy and the Gulf of Mexico. EddyFlow demonstrates that physics-informed representation learning reduces zero-shot RMSE by 21%, achieves up to 85.6% skill relative to persistence on unseen domains, and maintains near-ideal spectral fidelity with a PSD ratio of $\approx 1.00$.

</details>


### [147] [Physics-informed reduced-order modelling with equivariant spectral submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04239)
*Georg Maierhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等变谱子流形（eSSM）约简方法，将全阶模型的对称性显式纳入SSM框架，利用群作用加速高维系统的非线性降阶模型计算，在多个基准测试中展示速度和鲁棒性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM约简虽在数学上严谨且能捕捉超越线性方法（如DMD）的动力学，但其计算代价高昂，尤其对高维系统而言。

Method: 首先从数学上证明SSM是天然等变子流形，相关图表和约简动力学继承诱导群作用；在此基础上开发等变SSM约简算法，利用对称性实现更快的计算并提升模型鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个基准问题（包括Common Task Framework for Science的测试）上验证了该方法在计算速度和模型鲁棒性方面的优势。

Conclusion: eSSM约简通过显式利用对称性，在保持SSM数学原则性的同时显著提升了高维非线性降阶模型的计算效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Spectral submanifold (SSM) reduction has emerged as a mathematically principled route to reliable nonlinear reduced-order models, capturing dynamics beyond the reach of linear techniques such as Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD). The computation of SSMs, however, remains computationally expensive, particularly for high-dimensional systems. In this work, we introduce equivariant spectral submanifold (eSSM) reduction, a novel extension of the SSM framework that explicitly incorporates symmetries of the full-order model into the reduction process. We establish the mathematical foundations of this approach by showing that SSMs are naturally equivariant submanifolds and that the associated charts and reduced dynamics inherit the appropriate induced group actions. Building on this framework, we develop a novel equivariant SSM reduction algorithm that exploits these symmetries to achieve substantially faster computations while also improving model robustness. We demonstrate the advantages of this approach on several benchmark problems including a test from the Common Task Framework for Science.

</details>


### [148] [Attention-based representations for multi-task computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04243)
*Daniel Hsu,Mingyue Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多头注意力层在两种多任务场景中所需的注意力头数量下界。第一种场景中，两个小维度和低精度的注意力头就能同时计算列表中的最小值和最大值，但单个注意力头需要指数级更高的嵌入维度或精度。第二种场景中，计算n比特XOR需要注意力头数量与多项式阶数的乘积至少为n，并构造了匹配此下界的多头注意力层。结果推广到任意对称布尔函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力层能产生支持多种下游任务的向量表示，但关于所需注意力头数量的理论界尚不清楚。理解在多任务场景下需要多少个注意力头，对于理解Transformer架构的表达能力和设计高效模型具有重要意义。

Method: 本文通过两种具体场景建立注意力头数量的下界：(1) 使用向量表示使线性预测器同时计算列表中的最小值和最大值，证明单头注意力需要指数级更高的嵌入维度或精度（对比已知的双头即可实现的结果）；(2) 使用多项式阈值函数计算n比特XOR，证明注意力头数与多项式阶数的乘积必须至少为n，并构造匹配该下界的多头注意力层，进一步推广到任意对称布尔函数（以阈值度给出界）。

Result: 在最大/最小场景中，证明单个注意力头需要指数级更高的嵌入维度或比特精度，而两个注意力头只需小维度和低精度即可。在XOR场景中，证明注意力头数与多项式阶数乘积至少为n，并构造了达到此下界的注意力层；该结果推广到任意对称布尔函数，界由阈值度刻画。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上揭示了多头注意力在多任务场景中的必要性：单个注意力头在某些任务上需要指数级的资源补偿，而多个注意力头可以显著降低对嵌入维度和精度的需求。这些下界及其对应的构造为理解注意力机制的表达能力提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: Multi-head attention layers produce vector representations that support multiple downstream tasks. We establish bounds on the number of heads required in two simple and concrete multi-task scenarios. In the first scenario, a vector representation is sought so that linear predictors can compute both the smallest and largest numbers in a given list. In this case, it is known two attention heads with small embedding dimension and bit precision level suffice. We prove that a single attention head requires exponentially higher embedding dimension or precision level. In the second scenario, a vector representation is sought so that a polynomial threshold function can compute the XOR of a given string of $n$ bits. This scenario is analogous to the first one for $n=2$, since XOR is readily computed by a linear function using a vector representation that encodes both the AND and the OR of the two bits. We observe that $n$-bit XOR requires the product of the number of heads and the polynomial degree to be at least $n$, and we construct multi-head attention layers that match this lower bound. These results generalize to arbitrary (symmetric) Boolean functions, where the bound is given in terms of the threshold degree.

</details>


### [149] [Geometry-Informed Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Pre-trained Molecular GNNs for Blood-Brain Barrier Permeability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04257)
*Marco Vieto Vega,Long D. Nguyen,Binh P. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出BBBP-GeoPEFT框架，通过多截断距离图和线图编码三维几何信息，以轻量级参数高效微调方式（仅更新10.1%参数）实现血脑屏障通透性预测，在随机和骨架划分下达到与全参数微调及代表性PEFT基线相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血脑屏障通透性（BBBP）预测是中枢神经系统药物发现中的关键任务，但面临数据集有限、类别不平衡及分子结构敏感性等挑战。现有基于预训练分子GNN的全参数微调方法参数效率低且易过拟合，而现有PEFT方法主要适配节点特征或二维共价图，无法捕捉三维几何构象和第二阶层相互作用，限制了预测能力。

Method: BBBP-GeoPEFT框架包含以下核心设计：(1) 从分子构象构建多截断距离图及其对应的线图，以捕捉空间原子分布和第二阶边交互；(2) 使用轻量级辅助几何图编码器生成截断特化表示；(3) 通过节点级截断注意力机制和门控残差连接将几何信息注入预训练GNN的每一层；(4) 仅训练少量参数，保持预训练知识的同时引入通透性相关几何信息。

Result: 在构建的BBBP数据集上，BBBP-GeoPEFT在随机和骨架两种划分方式下，多数实验中达到与完全微调及代表性PEFT基线相当或更优的ROC-AUC和准确率，且仅更新模型10.1%的参数。

Conclusion: BBBP-GeoPEFT通过将三维几何信息和第二阶交互以参数高效方式注入预训练分子GNN，有效解决了BBBP预测中数据稀缺和微调过拟合的问题，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅减少训练参数量，验证了几何信息引导的PEFT策略在分子性质预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Blood-brain barrier permeability (BBBP) prediction is a critical screening task in central nervous system drug discovery, where candidate molecules must be assessed for whether they can cross, or should be prevented from crossing, the blood-brain barrier. However, this task remains challenging because of limited, class-imbalanced datasets and sensitivity to molecular structure. Recent advances in deep learning have established graph neural networks (GNNs) as a powerful approach for molecular representation learning, while pre-trained molecular GNNs provide transferable knowledge for downstream tasks. However, full fine-tuning is often parameter-inefficient and prone to overfitting, whereas existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods mainly adapt node features or the two-dimensional covalent graph, limiting their ability to capture three-dimensional geometry and second-order interactions. To address these limitations, we propose BBBP-GeoPEFT, a geometry-informed PEFT framework for pre-trained molecular GNNs. BBBP-GeoPEFT constructs distance-based graphs at multiple cutoffs and their corresponding line graphs from molecular conformers to capture spatial atom and second-order edge interactions. Lightweight auxiliary geometric graph encoders generate cutoff-specific representations, which are incorporated into each pre-trained layer through node-wise cutoff attention and gated residual connections. This design preserves pre-trained knowledge while incorporating permeability-relevant geometric information with a small trainable-parameter budget. Experiments on a curated BBBP dataset show that BBBP-GeoPEFT achieves competitive performance compared with full fine-tuning and representative PEFT baselines. Under both random and scaffold splitting, BBBP-GeoPEFT achieves competitive or improved ROC-AUC and accuracy in most experiments while updating only 10.1% of the model parameters.

</details>


### [150] [Sample Complexity of Multicalibration for Multilevel Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04288)
*Jiuyao Lu,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Aleksandr Podkopaev,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了序列化多属性校准（multicalibration for a sequence of k properties）的样本复杂度问题。针对任意固定k≥2，建立了匹配的上下界：多校准误差ε需要Ω̃(ε^(-(k+2)))样本；同时给出了使用O(ε^(-(k+2))+ε^(-2)log|𝒢|)样本的随机化学习器。因此对于多项式规模的组族，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^(-(k+2)))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多预测任务需要同时估计同一条件结果分布的多个相关特征（如均值、方差、偏度、条件风险价值等），这些特征之间存在层级依赖关系（每个属性在前序属性固定后才可识别）。现有多校准研究主要关注单一属性，缺乏对序列化多属性校准问题样本复杂度的系统分析，因此需要建立统一的理论框架并刻画其样本复杂度。

Method: 本文建立了k个属性序列化多校准的通用框架（类似Bayes对但不需要由单一损失函数产生）。技术上：1) 证明了下界——即使仅使用poly对数多个二元组，达到ε多校准误差也需要Ω̃(ε^(-(k+2)))样本；2) 构造了随机化学习器，对任意有限组族𝒢，样本复杂度为O(ε^(-(k+2))+ε^(-2)log|𝒢|)；3) 通过三个经典实例验证了理论。

Result: 对于固定k≥2，样本复杂度上下界匹配（除对数因子）：下界为Ω̃(ε^(-(k+2)))，上界为O(ε^(-(k+2))+ε^(-2)log|𝒢|)。对于多项式规模的组族，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^(-(k+2)))。该结果在三个经典例子（方差、偏度、条件风险价值等）中得到实例化验证。

Conclusion: 序列化k属性多校准的样本复杂度本质上由ε^(-(k+2))决定，与组族规模无关（在多项式规模的假设下）。这一结果填补了多校准理论中从单一属性到序列化多属性扩展的空白，为实际预测任务中同时估计多个相关分布特征提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Calibration requires a predictor to be unbiased after conditioning on its own predictions. Multicalibration asks for this guarantee simultaneously across a collection of groups. Many prediction tasks ask for several related features of the same conditional outcome distribution: variance is defined relative to the mean, skewness relative to both mean and variance, and conditional value at risk relative to a quantile. We study multicalibration for a sequence of $k$ properties in which each property is identifiable once the preceding properties are fixed. This framework includes Bayes pairs but does not require the properties to arise from a single loss.
  For every fixed $k\ge2$, we establish matching upper and lower sample-complexity bounds up to logarithmic factors under regularity conditions. Even with only polylogarithmically many binary groups, achieving multicalibration error $\varepsilon$ requires $\widetildeΩ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ samples. Conversely, for any finite group family $\mathcal G$, we give a randomized learner using $O(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)}+\varepsilon^{-2}\log|\mathcal G|)$ samples. Thus the sample complexity is $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ for polynomial-size group families. We instantiate the theory for three canonical examples.

</details>


### [151] [Adaptive Finite-Budget Training for CVaR Risk-Aware Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04305)
*Yifan Wu,Junjie Lei,Wenjie Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应训练控制器用于条件风险价值（CVaR）风险感知Q学习（RaQL），通过六个协调机制重新设计训练流程而非改变风险目标本身，在比特币日频交易任务上将平均经验CVaR Bellman残差降低约85%，并在考虑交易成本后达到0.9281的Sharpe比率和6.46%的最大回撤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险感知Q学习（RaQL）虽然是动态风险目标的免模型双重时间尺度估计器，但其有限预算行为脆弱：固定的内循环超参数会产生不稳定的价值估计、持续的Bellman残差和低效的样本复用。这严重制约了RaQL在实际金融应用中的可靠性。

Method: 该方法在不改变原始CVaR估计器和Bellman不动点的前提下，重新设计了训练程序，包含六个协调机制：(1) 逐单元内步长设定；(2) 外层速率匹配的衰减同步；(3) 对内层VaR类变量进行短期早期修正；(4) 先覆盖后贪婪的样本分配规则；(5) 对成熟内层估计进行渐进式后缀聚合；(6) 从在线可观测量中数据驱动地校准关键尺度。

Result: 在20个随机种子和856,000条内层转移样本上，控制器将平均经验CVaR Bellman残差相对于固定参数基线降低约85%（MeanBEQ：1.2202降至0.1854；MeanBEV：1.1624降至0.0535），并在不同CVaR水平、折扣因子和训练预算下保持稳定性。在样本外测试集上，学习策略达到0.9281的Sharpe比率和6.46%的最大回撤（扣除交易成本后）。虽然买入持有策略累计回报更高（35.43% vs 23.61%），但自适应策略实现了显著更低的波动率（9.57% vs 47.93%）、回撤和CVaR损失。

Conclusion: 仅对训练过程进行自适应的有限预算设计，而不改变风险目标本身，就能显著提升风险感知Q学习在金融应用中的可靠性和风险调整后表现。

Abstract: Risk-aware Q-learning (RaQL) provides a model-free, two-timescale estimator for dynamic risk objectives, but its finite-budget behavior remains fragile: fixed inner-loop hyperparameters can produce unstable value estimates, persistent Bellman residuals, and inefficient sample reuse. This paper proposes an adaptive training controller for Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) RaQL and evaluates it on a daily Bitcoin trading task. The controller preserves the original CVaR estimator and Bellman fixed point; instead, it redesigns the training procedure through six coordinated mechanisms: per-cell inner-step sizing, outer-rate-matched decay synchronization, a short early correction for the VaR-like inner variable, a coverage-first-then-greedy sample allocation rule, progressive suffix aggregation of mature inner estimates, and data-driven calibration of key scales from online-observable quantities. Across 20 random seeds and 856,000 inner-transition samples, the controller reduces the mean empirical CVaR Bellman residual by approximately 85% relative to the fixed-parameter baseline (MeanBEQ: 1.2202 to 0.1854; MeanBEV: 1.1624 to 0.0535) and maintains stability across CVaR levels, discount factors, and training budgets. On the chronological out-of-sample test set, the learned policy attains a Sharpe ratio of 0.9281 with a maximum drawdown of 6.46% after transaction costs. Although buy-and-hold yields a higher cumulative return (35.43% vs. 23.61%), the adaptive policy achieves far lower volatility (9.57% vs. 47.93%), drawdown, and CVaR loss. These results demonstrate that adaptive finite-budget training design, applied solely to the training procedure without altering the risk objective, can materially improve the reliability and risk-adjusted performance of risk-aware Q-learning in financial applications.

</details>


### [152] [ArborEnum: Decision Tree Rashomon Sets over Continuous Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04310)
*Zakk Heile,Hayden McTavish,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能够精确枚举连续特征决策树Rashomon集合的算法，并开发了近似枚举放松方法和任意时刻算法，在保持近完美召回率的同时比现有枚举方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rashomon效应指多个模型可以在同一任务上达到相近的性能，这对鲁棒性、特征重要性和可定制性有重要影响。虽然决策树是少数可以完全枚举Rashomon集合的模型类别，但现有的枚举计算依赖于对原始数据的二值化处理，这要么限制树的划分方式，要么显著增加本就困难的组合问题的复杂度。因此需要一种能够直接利用连续特征有序结构的精确枚举算法。

Method: 提出了第一个利用连续特征有序结构进行决策树Rashomon集合精确枚举的算法。同时开发了用于近似枚举的放松方法，以及一个任意时刻算法，后者能逐步细化候选阈值集合，产生越来越精细的近似结果并最终收敛到连续特征的Rashomon集合。

Result: 实验表明，粗粒度的二值化会遗漏大量树、重要特征和预测多样性。所提出算法比现有枚举方法实现了数量级的加速，近似方法在提供进一步加速的同时保持了近完美的召回率。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了连续特征决策树Rashomon集合精确枚举的空白，证明了利用连续特征的有序结构可以避免二值化带来的信息损失和计算开销，并在效率和完备性之间取得了较好的平衡。

Abstract: The Rashomon effect describes the phenomenon that many models can achieve nearly equivalent performance on the same learning task, with significant ramifications for robustness, feature importance, and customizability. These use cases motivate the computation of Rashomon sets: the set of all models whose regularized loss is near-optimal. Decision trees are one of the few model classes for which Rashomon sets can be fully enumerated, but this computation has always been conditional on a binarization of the original data, either restricting which splits each tree is allowed to make or substantially increasing the complexity of an already difficult combinatorial problem. We introduce the first algorithm that exactly enumerates decision-tree Rashomon sets while exploiting the ordered structure of continuous features. We further develop a relaxation for approximate enumeration and an anytime algorithm that progressively refines the set of candidate thresholds, producing increasingly detailed approximations that converge to the continuous-feature Rashomon set. Experiments show that coarse binarization can miss many trees, important features, and predictive multiplicity; our algorithms achieve orders-of-magnitude speedups over existing enumeration methods, with approximations providing further speedups while maintaining near-perfect recall.

</details>


### [153] [Efficient Online Lexicographic Generalized Low-Rank Matrix Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04324)
*Bo Xue,Ji Cheng,Haodong Jing,Hongzong Li,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有多个优先级目标的广义低秩矩阵臂老虎机问题，提出Lexi-LowGLM算法，通过在线牛顿步更新各目标估计器，将复杂度从O(T²)降至O(T)，并给出了依赖有效低秩维度的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩矩阵老虎机研究主要针对单一目标，而实际应用中多个目标往往具有不同优先级。此外，现有单目标算法使用批处理广义线性估计器，导致更新复杂度为O(T²)，在长时间范围内计算开销过大。

Method: 提出Lexi-LowGLM算法：(1) 先估计每个目标对应的低秩子空间；(2) 在降维后的特征空间中进行字典序学习。每个目标的估计器通过在线牛顿步更新，而非反复求解批处理估计器，从而将T轮内的估计器更新复杂度从O(T²)降至O(T)。

Result: 对每个目标i∈[m]建立了遗憾界Õ(W_i^{lex}·√m·(d1+d2)r·√T)，其中r为目标参数矩阵秩的上界，W_i^{lex}刻画字典序权衡效应。该遗憾界依赖有效低秩维度(d1+d2)r而非环境维度d1·d2，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性与计算效率。

Conclusion: Lexi-LowGLM是首个高效处理多优先级低秩矩阵老虎机问题的算法，在保持低秩维度依赖的遗憾界的同时，显著降低了计算复杂度，兼顾了理论保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: This paper studies generalized low-rank matrix bandits with multiple prioritized objectives. At each round, the learner selects a matrix-valued arm and observes a vector-valued reward, whose components correspond to multiple objectives with different priority levels. Each objective is governed by an objective-specific generalized low-rank matrix model, and the learner evaluates arms according to a lexicographic preference order, prioritizing higher-level objectives before lower-level ones. We propose \textsc{Lexi-LowGLM}, an efficient online algorithm that first estimates objective-specific low-rank subspaces and then performs lexicographic learning in the reduced feature spaces. Unlike existing single-objective algorithms that repeatedly solve a batch generalized linear estimator using all historical observations, \textsc{Lexi-LowGLM} updates each objective-specific estimator via an online Newton step, reducing the estimator-update complexity over $T$ rounds from $O(T^2)$ to $O(T)$. We establish a regret bound of $\widetilde O\left(W_i^{\rm lex}\sqrt{m}\,(d_1+d_2)r\sqrt{T}\right)$ for each objective $i\in[m]$, where $r$ is an upper bound on the ranks of the objective-specific parameter matrices and $W_i^{\rm lex}$ characterizes the lexicographic trade-off effect. This bound depends on the effective low-rank dimension $(d_1+d_2)r$ rather than the ambient dimension $d_1d_2$. Numerical experiments further validate the effectiveness and computational efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [154] [Real-time probabilistic tsunami forecasting via generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04327)
*Yusuke Oishi,Takashi Furumura,Fumihiko Imamura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件扩散模型（生成式AI）的概率集成模型，用于近海啸（如海沟型大地震引发的近场海啸）的陆上淹没预测，弥补了传统确定性预测缺乏不确定性量化的缺陷，并以2011年东北-冲绳地震数据验证了模型在预测淹没深度和范围的同时能够忠实追踪地震后随时间递减的不确定性，为下一代海啸预警系统提供了从确定性向概率性转变的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前海啸预警主要关注沿岸海啸高度而非陆上淹没范围，因为确定性预测的淹没边界在高不确定性条件下（如近场海啸）可能给人虚假的安全感；虽然机器学习可实现即时淹没预测，但仍是确定性的，缺乏不确定性量化，无法反映预测的置信程度。

Method: 开发了一个基于条件扩散模型的概率集成预测框架，条件扩散模型是一种生成式AI，能够生成多个可能的淹没情景样本，从而自然地输出概率分布而非单一确定值，在保证预测精度的同时实现概率校准（calibration）。

Result: 使用2011年东北-冲绳大地震数据进行验证，模型能够准确预测淹没深度和淹没范围，同时能够忠实追踪地震后时间推移过程中不确定性的递减过程，实现了预测精度与概率校准之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 该工作表明生成式AI可以将海啸预报从确定性范式转向概率性范式，为新一代海啸早期预警系统奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Explicit onshore tsunami inundation forecasting can improve public risk awareness, but deterministically predicted inundation boundaries under highly uncertain conditions, such as near-field tsunamis generated by megathrust earthquakes, may falsely imply safety outside the boundaries. Consequently, current warnings primarily target coastal tsunami height, not onshore inundation. Although machine learning enables instant inundation predictions, they remain deterministic, lacking uncertainty quantification. Here, we develop a probabilistic ensemble model based on a conditional diffusion model (a type of generative AI) that reconciles accuracy with calibration. Validated with the 2011 Tohoku-oki earthquake data, our model faithfully tracks the postearthquake uncertainty decreasing over time while accurately predicting inundation depth and extent. Our framework shows that generative AI can shift tsunami forecasting from determinism to probabilism, providing a foundation for next-generation early warning.

</details>


### [155] [Cost-Aware Multi-Objective Bandits: Theory and Application to Budgeted LLM Configuration Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04333)
*Bo Xue,Zhi Hong,Jiayi Li,Yuanyu Wan,Ji Cheng,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM配置评估建模为成本感知的多目标赌博机问题，分别针对在线配置选择和Pareto配置识别两个基本问题提出了基于超体积的UCB算法和成本感知的经验间隙消除算法，并给出了相应的理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM配置评估面临评估预算有限、成本各异以及多个竞争目标并存等挑战，现有方法难以在有限预算下高效地进行配置选择和Pareto最优配置识别。

Method: 将LLM配置评估形式化为成本感知的多目标赌博机问题，每个配置评估产生配置相关的成本和带噪声的向量值结果。针对在线配置选择提出基于超体积的UCB算法，优化乐观的超体积-成本比指标；针对固定预算的Pareto识别提出成本感知的经验间隙消除算法。

Result: 在线配置选择算法达到O(∑_{i≠i*}(log B)/Δ_i)的预算后悔界，保持了经典单目标赌博机的对数预算依赖；Pareto识别算法的错误概率为O(exp(-B/H_{μ,c}))，随评估预算指数衰减，并在所有配置成本相同时恢复标准Pareto集合识别保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在有限预算下实现了高效的LLM配置在线决策和准确的成本感知Pareto识别，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) configuration evaluation is challenging due to limited evaluation budgets, varying costs, and multiple competing objectives. In this paper, we formulate LLM configuration evaluation as a cost-aware multi-objective bandit problem, where each configuration evaluation incurs a configuration-dependent cost and yields a noisy vector-valued outcome. Under this framework, we study two fundamental problems: online configuration selection and Pareto configuration identification. For online configuration selection, we propose a hypervolume-based UCB algorithm that optimizes an optimistic hypervolume-per-cost index. We establish a budgeted regret bound of order $O\bigl(\sum_{i\ne i^\star}\frac{\log B}{Δ_i}\bigr)$, where $B$ is the evaluation budget, $i^\star$ is the optimal configuration in terms of hypervolume efficiency, and $Δ_i$ is the corresponding efficiency gap of configuration $i$. This bound retains the logarithmic budget dependence of classical single-objective budgeted bandits. For fixed-budget Pareto identification, we develop a cost-aware empirical gap elimination algorithm and prove that its error probability is of order $O\bigl(\exp(-\frac{B}{H_{μ,c}})\bigr)$, where $H_{μ,c}$ is a cost-aware Pareto identification complexity depending on configuration costs and Pareto classification gaps. This error probability decays exponentially with the evaluation budget and recovers the standard Pareto set identification guarantee when all configuration costs are identical. Experiments on LLM configuration evaluation tasks demonstrate that the proposed framework enables efficient online decision-making and accurate cost-aware Pareto identification under limited budgets.

</details>


### [156] [ATLAS: Adaptive Topological Learning with Abstract Successors for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04334)
*R. Blake Lawlor,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS算法，结合Grow When Required网络与Successor Features，在非平稳环境中实现高样本效率和抗遗忘能力，并通过结构化解耦转移动力学与奖励信号实现近即时的新目标适应和正向反向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模型强化学习算法性能高但样本效率低且对环境变化不鲁棒；基于模型的算法虽样本效率高，但在环境变化时仍会失效。需要一种兼具高样本效率和鲁棒性的算法来应对非平稳环境中的挑战（尤其是灾难性遗忘问题）。

Method: ATLAS采用Grow When Required（GWR）网络与Successor Features相结合。GWR网络以增量拓扑学习方式建模环境状态空间，实现高样本效率；Successor Features将转移动力学与奖励信号解耦，使得在新目标变化时无需重新学习环境模型，从而快速适应并缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在空间导航任务中，ATLAS相比常见的on-policy和off-policy基线算法，在非平稳环境中显著优于各基线方法；通过解耦转移动力学与奖励信号，实现了对新目标的近即时适应，并展现出正向反向迁移能力。

Conclusion: 结构化解耦转移动力学与奖励信号是提高强化学习算法在非平稳环境中鲁棒性和适应能力的关键。ATLAS证明了将增量拓扑学习与Successor Features结合能够在保持高样本效率的同时有效对抗灾难性遗忘，为应对环境漂移的强化学习算法设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Contemporary model-free reinforcement learning algorithms can achieve very high performance, but have low sample efficiency and are not robust to changes in the environment. Model-based algorithms have much higher sample efficiency, but still fail when the environment shifts. This paper introduces Adaptive Topological Learning with Abstract Successors (ATLAS) to combat these challenges. ATLAS uses a Grow When Required network with Successor Features in order to achieve high sample efficiency while also robustly tackling catastrophic forgetting. We evaluate ATLAS in spatial navigation tasks, benchmarking its performance against common on-policy and off-policy algorithms. Our empirical results demonstrate that by structurally decoupling transition dynamics from the reward signal, ATLAS achieves near-instantaneous adaptation to new goals and can exhibit positive backward transfer, significantly outperforming baseline methods in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [157] [Looking in the Mirror: Introspecting Side-Effect Misalignments Induced by Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04347)
*Kotaro Yoshida,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Yukinori Yamamoto,Yuji Naraki,Ryotaro Shimizu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出侧效应内省（Side-Effect Introspection）问题设置，研究微调带来的意外对齐退化而非显式植入的行为，并构建了相应数据集。同时提出Delta感知内省适配器（DAIA），通过显式处理基座模型激活与微调引起的激活差异来提升对内部变化的敏感性，实验证明其优于现有内省适配器并具备跨模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内省适配器研究主要针对显式植入行为的解释，而实际部署中更关心的是微调导致的意外对齐退化（side-effect misalignment），即在与安全或对齐无直接关联的任务上微调时发生的非预期对齐属性破坏。现有方法在数据分布和问题设定上与真实场景存在差距。

Method: 提出侧效应内省问题设定并构建对应数据集；提出DAIA（Delta-Aware Introspection Adapter），该机制同时处理基础模型激活与微调引起的激活差异（delta），从而增强对模型内部变化的敏感性。

Result: 实验表明内省学习能够泛化到未见过的微调模型和安全类别，DAIA在性能上持续优于现有内省适配器。

Conclusion: 将内省任务从显式植入行为扩展到侧效应内省是可行且有价值的，DAIA通过显式建模激活差异有效提高了内省的敏感性，为实际部署场景中的对齐退化检测提供了新方法。

Abstract: Fine-tuning enables a source model to acquire desired capabilities and behaviors in a target domain while retaining much of its general-purpose competence. However, this adaptation process can also degrade alignment properties that were present in the source model. Recent work has shown that large language models can be trained using LoRA-based modules known as introspection adapters (IAs) to describe behavioral changes induced by fine-tuning. However, existing studies primarily consider settings in which the model is fine-tuned on datasets explicitly designed to implant a specific behavior and is then asked to explain the implanted behavior. This differs from practical deployment scenarios, where the central concern is often side-effect misalignment: unintended degradation of alignment caused by fine-tuning on tasks that are not obviously related to safety or alignment. To bridge this gap, we formulate a novel problem setting called \emph{side-effect introspection}, in which the target of introspection is not a behavior explicitly implanted through fine-tuning, but rather alignment shifts that emerge as unintended side effects, and we construct a dataset for this setting. Furthermore, to enhance sensitivity to internal model changes, we propose the Delta-Aware Introspection Adapter (DAIA), a novel mechanism designed to explicitly process both base-model activations and activation differences induced by fine-tuning. Our empirical evaluation shows that introspection learning generalizes to unseen fine-tuned models and safety categories, and that DAIA consistently outperforms existing introspection adapters.

</details>


### [158] [Manipulation-Proof Oblivious Audits against Deceptive Model Providers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04365)
*Augustin Godinot,Sofiane Azogagh,Julien Ferry,Sébastien Gambs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于私有信息检索（PIR）的新型审计协议，通过让审计者以 oblivious 方式查询模型，显著提高模型提供者操纵审计结果的事后可检测性。该协议效率高、对审计者开销极小，且无需修改被审计模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法审计是AI治理的关键工具，但审计过程容易被模型提供者操纵——尤其在监管场景下审计通常可被察觉，提供者可以推断敏感属性并在群体间策略性地均衡分配率以满足公平性指标，从而掩盖不公平行为。现有审计方法无法有效防御这种操纵。

Method: 利用私有信息检索（PIR）机制，审计者要求提供者标注大量实例，但提供者无法知晓哪些子集最终会被用于审计。这使得提供者若试图掩盖不公平，必须伪造远超正常数量的响应，从而增大操纵难度和被发现的可能性。协议在理论上提供了保证，且不改变模型本身。

Result: 理论分析表明该协议能显著增加操纵者伪造响应的数量要求，提升事后可检测性；跨多个代表性审计场景的实验结果验证了方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该审计协议为公平性审计提供了更强的防操纵保障，效率高、兼容现有模型部署，为算法治理中的审计完整性提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Audits have emerged as a critical instrument for algorithmic governance, providing a mechanism for external scrutiny and governance of machine learning models. However, ensuring the integrity of such assessments remains a challenging issue. For instance in regulatory contexts, audits are typically declared or easily detected, thus enabling model providers to manipulate the process, whether intentionally or inadvertently. This vulnerability is particularly acute in the context of fairness evaluations, in which providers can often infer sensitive attributes and strategically equalize allocation rates between groups to satisfy fairness metrics.
  In this paper, we introduce a novel audit protocol designed to significantly increase the post-audit detectability of such manipulations by enabling the auditor to query the model in an oblivious manner. Our approach leverages a Private Information Retrieval mechanism to require the provider to label a large set of instances, while preventing it from knowing which subset will ultimately be used for the audit. The protocol is efficient, imposes minimal overhead on the auditor, and requires no modification to the audited model, its training procedure, or its inference pipeline. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that, under this protocol, a provider attempting to hide unfairness must falsify a significantly larger number of responses, thereby increasing both the difficulty and the likelihood of detection of manipulation. Experimental results across representative audit scenarios confirm the effectiveness and practicality of our approach.

</details>


### [159] [EvtGraph: Event-Adaptive Compression for Sparse Temporal Graph Learning in Multimodal Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04368)
*Ziqian Wang,Tingxiong Xiao,Yuxiao Cheng,Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvtGraph，一个统一的预算约束框架，通过事件自适应压缩、节点预算选择和时序约束稀疏图推理，将密集多模态时序数据转换为基于显著事件的结构化计算，在降低复杂度的同时保持关键转换信息，实验证明其优于基于Transformer和循环神经网络的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态时序数据天然具有不规则性和信息密度不均匀的特点，但现有模型大多依赖均匀离散化，导致表示效率低下、计算资源浪费。

Method: EvtGraph包含三个核心模块：(1) 事件自适应压缩（EAMC）将序列重参数化为事件级token；(2) 节点预算选择（NBC）在显式预算约束下选取紧凑子集；(3) 时序约束稀疏图推理（T2SG）在显著事件上进行结构化推理，从而将密集序列转化为受约束的计算过程。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV+CXR多模态临床基准和跨领域基准上，EvtGraph在性能上优于Transformer和循环基线，同时显著提升计算效率；小预算在多数实际场景中已足够，表现出一致的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 预算约束下的事件中心表示为从高冗余时序数据中学习提供了通用范式，表明将计算与时间显著性对齐是提高效率和性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal temporal data are inherently irregular and uneven in information density, yet most models rely on uniform discretization, leading to inefficient representations.
  We propose \textbf{EvtGraph}, a unified framework that aligns computation with temporal salience under explicit budget constraints. EvtGraph reparameterizes sequences into event-level tokens via event-adaptive compression (EAMC), selects a compact subset with a node budget (NBC), and performs temporally constrained sparse graph reasoning (T2SG). This transforms dense sequences into structured computation over salient events, reducing complexity while preserving critical transitions.
  We show that this design provides a practical mechanism for allocating representational capacity under a fixed budget, yielding a consistent performance--efficiency trade-off, where a small budget is often sufficient in practice. Experiments on multimodal clinical (MIMIC-IV + CXR) and cross-domain benchmarks demonstrate that EvtGraph outperforms both Transformer-based and recurrent baselines while significantly improving efficiency.
  These results suggest that budget-constrained event-centric representation provides a general paradigm for learning from high-redundancy temporal data.

</details>


### [160] [Towards Trustworthy Hypergraph Neural Networks under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04377)
*Mengyao Zhou,Zhiheng Zhou,Xiao Han,Guiying Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了超图节点分类中的标签噪声问题，提出了统一的基准测试，并设计了HyperTrust鲁棒学习框架，通过估计超边可信度、增强（HyperedgeBoost）和剪枝（HyperedgePrune）两个模块协同调整超图结构，在多数据集和多噪声设置下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图神经网络（HGNNs）在处理高阶关系方面表现出色，但其性能高度依赖标注数据，容易受到标签噪声的影响。虽然已有许多针对普通图和一般学习的标签噪声处理方法（LLN和GLN），但这些方法在超图上的适用性尚不明确，超图场景下的噪声标签学习仍是一个未被充分探索的问题。

Method: 首先，将代表性的LLN和GLN方法适配到超图场景，并在统一基准下评估，揭示现有鲁棒学习策略在超图上的局限性。在此基础上提出HyperTrust框架，包含两个核心模块：（1）基于预训练和熵感知策略估计超边可信度；（2）HyperedgeBoost模块通过将未标注节点连接到可信超边以增强可靠的监督信号，以及HyperedgePrune模块通过移除不可信节点-超边关联来抑制噪声传播。两个模块协同调整超图结构并生成最终预测。

Result: 大量实验和理论分析表明，HyperTrust在多个超图数据集和多种噪声设置下均展现出有效性和鲁棒性，优于适配后的LLN和GLN基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了超图标签噪声学习的统一基准和有效解决方案，为该方向未来的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Hypergraph neural networks (HGNNs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in processing complex higher-order relationships. However, their performance is highly dependent on labeled data, making them vulnerable to label noise. Despite advances in learning with label noise (LLN) and graph learning with label noise (GLN), noisy-label learning on hypergraphs remains underexplored. In this paper, we present a systematic study of hypergraph node classification under label noise. First, we adapt representative LLN and GLN methods to hypergraphs and evaluate them under a unified benchmark, revealing the limitations of existing robust learning strategies for hypergraphs. Building on this, we propose a new hypergraph robust framework, HyperTrust, which first estimates hyperedge trustworthiness through a pretraining-based, entropy-aware strategy, and then incorporates the HyperedgeBoost module to enhance reliable supervision by connecting unlabeled nodes to trustworthy hyperedges, as well as the HyperedgePrune module to suppress noisy propagation by removing untrustworthy node-hyperedge incidences. Finally, two modules work collaboratively to adjust the hypergraph structure and generate final predictions. Extensive experiments and theoretical analysis demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of HyperTrust on multiple hypergraph datasets under various noisy settings. Our work provides a unified benchmark and an effective solution for hypergraph learning with label noise and lays a foundation for future research in this direction.

</details>


### [161] [NodeJEPA: Structure-Conditioned Latent Prediction for Node-Level Graph Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04381)
*Tinghe Zhang,Jian Xu,Jiaheng Chen,Jiaxing Li,Yucheng Xiao,Qiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NodeJEPA是一种用于节点级图自监督学习的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）。它通过对结构感知的k跳自我子图进行掩码，并使用EMA更新的目标编码器生成潜在目标，让上下文编码器在潜在空间中预测掩码节点的表示，而非在输入空间重建。结构条件预测器通过交叉注意力整合光谱与中心性描述符，并结合方差、协方差和拉普拉斯谱正则化器稳定嵌入几何。该方法不依赖输入重建或手工设计的图增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图自监督学习方法主要有两大范式：对比学习方法依赖精心设计的增强，生成式方法在输入空间重构节点属性。两者都可能使表示与低层输入统计纠缠而非与关系结构对齐。虽然近期工作探索了图级JEPA，但如何在节点级任务上设计JEPA式目标、预测器应基于哪些结构信号仍不清楚，需要系统研究。

Method: NodeJEPA包含以下核心组件：(1) 掩码结构感知的k跳自我子图；(2) EMA更新的目标编码器提供潜在目标，带stop-gradient；(3) 结构条件预测器通过交叉注意力整合光谱与中心性描述符；(4) 方差、协方差和拉普拉斯谱正则化器稳定嵌入几何；(5) 可选的课程学习逐渐增加掩码难度。整体在潜在空间中预测，不依赖输入重建或手工图增强。

Result: 在标准节点分类基准上，NodeJEPA在linear probing和fine-tuning协议下进行了评估，并开展了关于掩码策略、预测目标和正则化设计的消融实验。结果表明NodeJEPA为节点级JEPA式潜在预测提供了实用配方，并阐明了结构条件化何时有助于表示学习。

Conclusion: NodeJEPA为图上的节点级自监督学习提供了一种新的JEPA式范式，通过潜在空间中的结构条件预测而非输入重建或对比增强来学习关系结构。该工作为节点级JEPA设计提供了实践经验，并揭示了结构信号条件化在表示学习中的作用。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning on graphs is largely shaped by contrastive methods that depend on carefully designed augmentations, and by generative methods that reconstruct node attributes in the input space. Both paradigms can entangle representations with low-level input statistics rather than with relational structure. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPA) instead learn by predicting latent targets rather than reconstructing inputs. Recent work has explored this idea for graph-level representation learning, but how to design JEPA-style objectives for node-level tasks, and which structural signals the predictor should condition on, remains less clear. We present NodeJEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture for node-level graph self-supervised learning. NodeJEPA masks structure-aware k-hop ego-subgraphs and trains a context encoder to predict the latent representations of the masked nodes. These targets come from an EMA-updated target encoder with stop-gradient. A structure-conditioned predictor integrates spectral and centrality descriptors through cross-attention. Variance, covariance, and Laplacian spectral regularizers help stabilize the embedding geometry, and an optional curriculum gradually increases masking difficulty during training. Because prediction occurs in latent space, NodeJEPA does not rely on input reconstruction or hand-crafted graph augmentations. We evaluate NodeJEPA on standard node classification benchmarks under linear probing and fine-tuning protocols, and conduct ablations on masking, prediction, and regularization design choices. Our study offers a practical recipe for node-level JEPA-style latent prediction on graphs, and clarifies when structural conditioning helps representation learning. Code, configurations, and evaluation scripts are publicly available at https://github.com/OliverZ-dot/Node-Jepa.

</details>


### [162] [Non-asymptotic implicit bias of logistic regression at early-stage gradient descent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04382)
*Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究梯度下降中参数向量与最大间隔方向的早期弱对齐现象。理论证明在O(exp(exp(-δ)))次迭代内，参数向量即可与最大间隔方向弱对齐（δ为允许的对齐误差），该边界被证明是紧的。与传统的渐近收敛率（显著慢于纯凸优化）不同，该早期对齐机制通过直接追踪径向和切向流动来建立，绕过了渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降中参数向量的隐式偏差（如最大间隔偏差）是防止过拟合的关键机制，但最大间隔收敛是渐近现象，其收敛速度远慢于纯凸优化。然而实践中参数向量在远少于渐近率的迭代次数内就与最大间隔方向正相关。理解这一早期对齐阶段的内在机制是本文的核心动机。

Method: 通过追踪参数向量的径向（radial）和切向（tangential）流动，直接对对齐动力学进行分析，利用数据集几何性质，避免了传统渐近展开方法。具体而言，将参数向量分解为沿最大间隔方向的径向分量和与之垂直的切向分量，通过分析两者的动态演化来建立弱对齐的有限时间界。

Result: 理论上证明了参数向量在O(exp(exp(-δ)))次迭代内即可与最大间隔方向实现弱对齐，其中δ>0为允许的对齐误差，且该迭代边界被证明是紧的（tight）。

Conclusion: 本文为梯度下降的早期阶段对齐现象提供了理论解释，表明参数向量在远早于渐近收敛的时间内即可朝最大间隔方向弱对齐，这解释了"训练越久泛化越好"的经验观察中早期阶段的行为，且证明了该弱对齐速率是渐近最优的。

Abstract: Gradient descent has been of particular interest in modern machine learning beyond sole focus on optimization. Implicit bias emerging from optimization, though not being encoded by the learning objective, often prevents from overfitting to spurious patterns. A typical instance is the max-margin implicit bias of a linear classifier, widely established for exponentially tailed loss functions. Even after having a given dataset separated, the parameter vector continues to evolve towards the max-margin direction asymptotically along the gradient descent dynamics. This phenomenon corroborates a frequent empirical observation of "train longer, generalize better." However, the max-margin convergence is an asymptotic phenomenon, and what is worse, this asymptotic convergence rate is significantly slower than pure convex optimization. Even so, the parameter vector along gradient descent dynamics commonly correlates with the max-margin direction positively (though not exactly) within considerably fewer iterations than the asymptotic rate. By shedding another light on this classical problem, this work aims to understand the mechanism of this early-stage alignment phenomenon. Our theoretical results demonstrate that the parameter vector weakly aligns with the max-margin direction within $O(\exp(\exp(-δ)))$ iterations, where $δ>0$ is the permissible alignment error, which is shown to be tight. By tracking the radial and tangential flows, our proof operates on the alignment dynamics directly with dataset geometry and gets rid of the asymptotic expansion, which is a key insight to establishing faster weak alignment.

</details>


### [163] [NeuroPB: Scaling Neural Decoding with Pretrained Behavioral Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04389)
*Luyao Jin,Yonghao Song,Huan Zhao,Vincent C. K. Cheung,Wei-Hsin Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroPB框架，通过在大规模行为数据上预训练运动编码器，再将神经活动与行为表征空间对齐，实现神经解码的规模化。实验表明该框架在猕猴运动数据集上显著提升轨迹解码性能（中心向外任务R²提升11%，随机目标任务提升8%），且机器人预训练数据可替代生物数据，为数据稀缺条件下的高性能BCI提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经解码器受限于神经记录数据规模小、异质性高的瓶颈，而行为数据可从人类、动物、仿真和机器人系统大规模获取。因此，研究者希望利用丰富的预训练行为表征来扩展神经解码能力，缓解神经数据稀缺的问题。

Method: NeuroPB框架分三步：1）在大规模运动行为数据上预训练运动编码器；2）利用有限的配对神经-行为记录将神经活动对齐到行为表征空间；3）联合优化神经编码器和轻量运动解码器，从对齐后的神经表征重建连续运动轨迹。

Result: 在多个猕猴运动数据集上，行为预训练相比从头训练运动编码器，中心向外任务R²提升11%，随机目标任务提升8%；机器人轨迹预训练与猕猴轨迹预训练效果相当；随机器人预训练数据的规模与多样性增加，解码性能持续提升；仅需10%标定数据即可达到从头训练的性能，并增强了跨记录会话、跨被试和跨运动任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 行为预训练为神经解码提供了可扩展的数据来源，验证了生物与人工系统之间共享的可迁移运动学结构，为神经数据有限条件下实现高性能、低标定成本的脑机接口指明了可行方向。

Abstract: Decoding continuous motor trajectories from neural activity is essential for developing practical brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). However, current neural decoders are constrained by the limited scale and heterogeneity of neural recordings. In contrast, behavioral data can be collected more readily and at substantially larger scale from humans, animals, simulations, and robotic systems. Here, we introduce NeuroPB, a framework that scales neural decoding by transferring knowledge from pretrained behavioral representations. NeuroPB first pretrains a motor encoder on large-scale motor behavior data and then aligns neural activity with the resulting behavioral representation space using a limited set of paired neural-behavioral recordings. A neural encoder and lightweight motor decoder are subsequently optimized to reconstruct continuous movement from the aligned neural representations. Across multiple macaque motor datasets, behavioral pretraining improves trajectory decoding, including an 11% $R^2$ increase on center-out and 8% on random-target compared with training the motor encoder from scratch. Notably, pretraining on robotic trajectories achieves performance comparable to pretraining on macaque trajectories, demonstrating that transferable kinematic structure is shared across biological and artificial models. Moreover, decoding performance improves as the scale and diversity of robotic pretraining data increase, when the amount of neural data is fixed. Pretraining also enhances generalization across recording sessions, subjects, and motor tasks, with only 10% calibration needed to match training from scratch. Overall, these results establish behavioral pretraining as a scalable source for neural decoding and provide a promising route toward high-performance and calibration-efficient BCIs under limited neural data.

</details>


### [164] [When Proxy Prediction Becomes Equation Reconstruction: Diagnostics and Residual Learning for Factor-Derived Proxy Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04393)
*Chayan Lahiri,Ahmed Shafee,Cody Fehringer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Scientific machine learning often relies on proxy targets computed from known domain factors when direct observations are limited. When those same factors are used as model inputs, however, high predictive accuracy may reflect reconstruction of the proxy-generating equation rather than robustness to degraded factor information. We study this problem in RUSLE-derived soil-loss proxy prediction under controlled degradation of the soil-erodibility factor $K$. We introduce a diagnostic framework that combines degraded-formula references, classical tree-based baselines, matched direct and formula-feature predictors, contextual ablations, tail-error analysis, and degradation robustness scoring. We then propose RASPL, a formula-preserving residual framework that retains the degraded formula estimate as the prediction anchor and learns an adaptively gated contextual correction. RASPL substantially outperforms matched direct prediction and provides stronger degradation and tail robustness than treating the formula estimate as an ordinary input feature. Within RASPL, a compact statistical encoder achieves the highest macro-averaged $R^2$ and lowest computational cost, whereas a convolutional encoder achieves the strongest degradation robustness and lowest Tail95 mean absolute error (MAE). These results establish formula preservation as the central design principle for robust learning from factor-derived proxy targets.

</details>


### [165] [Elbow-Based MoE Routing: A Training-Free Inference Time Plugin for Expert Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04401)
*Robin Pan,Raymond Liu,Daniel Fang,Adelina Andrei,Rosa Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于肘部检测的动态专家路由方法，无需训练即可在推理时按token动态调整专家数量，在保持精度的同时将延迟平均降低5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE模型使用固定的top-k路由策略，无论每个token需要多少个专家，都消耗相同的计算资源。这种固定计算策略忽略了不同token对专家数量的实际需求差异，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 提出elbow-based routing方法：作为推理时、无需训练的修改方案，检查排序后的路由概率分布，识别区分高概率与低概率专家的肘点，从而按token动态调整激活的专家数量。从理论和实验两方面证明该方法能保持专家负载均衡。

Result: 在最新MoE模型上的实验表明，该方法在六个基准测试上保持精度的同时，实现了平均5.3%的延迟降低。并且发现大多数路由分布存在清晰的可用于该策略的拐点。

Conclusion: 通过利用路由分布的固有肘部结构，可以在不牺牲精度的前提下实现推理效率提升，为MoE模型的动态计算分配提供了一种简洁有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable model scaling while maintaining low inference-time compute by activating only a subset of experts per token. However, conventional routing relies on a fixed top-k selection, forcing the model to spend the same compute regardless of how many experts are relevant. We introduce elbow-based routing, a training-free inference-time modification that dynamically adjusts the number of experts on a per-token basis. Our method examines the sorted router probability distribution and identifies an elbow point that separates high- and low-probability experts. We find that most router distributions exhibit clear inflection points suitable for this strategy, and we show both theoretically and empirically that elbow-based routing preserves expert load balance. Experiments on a state-of-the-art MoE model demonstrate an average latency reduction of 5.3% while maintaining accuracy across six benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [Training-Free Hashing-Based Attention via Binary Principal Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04405)
*Daohai Yu,Zhanpeng Zeng,Keyu Chen,Wenhao Li,Zhifeng Shen,Luxi Lin,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BinaryPC是一种无需训练、基于数据感知哈希的稀疏注意力机制，通过计算数据的二元主成分来构建紧凑的二元哈希码，在保持长上下文LLM精度的同时显著提升解码速度，实现端到端解码吞吐量3.56倍的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型在真实应用中部署日益增多，但自注意力机制仍是主要效率瓶颈，尤其是在解码阶段需要反复处理不断增长的KV缓存。现有稀疏注意力方法虽然通过关注更少的KV对来减少计算，但往往面临精度严重下降、需要额外训练或依赖昂贵哈希计算等问题。

Method: BinaryPC提出一种无训练、数据感知的哈希稀疏注意力机制。该方法通过计算数据的二元主成分来构建紧凑的二元哈希码及对应的哈希函数。与依赖数据无关随机投影的局部敏感哈希（LSH）或需要学习的非线性哈希方法不同，BinaryPC构建的二元码显式保留了数据的结构信息，且无需基于梯度的训练。

Result: 在多个模型家族和长上下文基准上的综合实验表明，BinaryPC在保持与全注意力相近精度的同时，在稀疏和哈希基方法中取得了优越性能。在现代GPU上，BinaryPC相比FlashAttention内核将端到端解码吞吐量提升了3.56倍。

Conclusion: BinaryPC通过数据感知的二元主成分哈希在无需训练的前提下实现了高效的稀疏注意力，兼顾了精度与计算效率，为长上下文LLM的解码阶段提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Long-context large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, yet self-attention remains a major efficiency bottleneck -- especially during decoding -- due to the necessity of repeatedly processing ever-growing key-value (KV) caches. Existing sparse attention reduce computation by attending to fewer KV pairs, but often suffer from substantial accuracy degradation, require additional training, or rely on expensive hashing. In this work, we present BinaryPC, a training-free, data-aware hashing-based sparse attention for long-context LLMs. BinaryPC constructs compact binary hash codes and corresponding hash function by computing binary principal components of data.
